                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM expenses WHERE id = ?", (entry_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()

            return self.get_all()

    def update_settings(self, new_settings):
        with self.lock:
            filtered_settings = {}
            for k, v in (new_settings or {}).items():
                if k == "hasAuth":
                    continue
                if k in ("adminPassword", "cashierPin") and (v is None or str(v).strip() == ""):
                    continue
                filtered_settings[k] = v

            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        for k, v in filtered_settings.items():
                            cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO settings (setting_key, setting_val)
                            VALUES (%s, %s)
                            ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE setting_val = VALUES(setting_val)
                            """, (k, json.dumps(v)))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    for k, v in filtered_settings.items():
                        cur.execute("""
                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (setting_key, setting_val)
                        VALUES (?, ?)
                        """, (k, json.dumps(v)))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            return self.get_all()

    def save_discount(self, discount_data):
        with self.lock:
            disc_id = (discount_data.get("id") or "").strip()
            if not disc_id:
                disc_id = generate_uid("DSC")
                discount_data["id"] = disc_id

            name = discount_data.get("name", "")
            code = (discount_data.get("code") or "").strip().upper()
            disc_type = discount_data.get("type", "percentage")
            value = to_rupiah(discount_data.get("value", 0))
            min_purchase = to_rupiah(discount_data.get("minPurchase", 0))
            max_discount = to_rupiah(discount_data.get("maxDiscount", 0))
            is_active = 1 if discount_data.get("isActive", True) else 0

            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO discounts (id, name, code, type, value, minPurchase, maxDiscount, isActive)
                        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                        ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                            name=VALUES(name), code=VALUES(code), type=VALUES(type),
                            value=VALUES(value), minPurchase=VALUES(minPurchase),
                            maxDiscount=VALUES(maxDiscount), isActive=VALUES(isActive)
                        """, (disc_id, name, code, disc_type, value, min_purchase, max_discount, is_active))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("""
                    INSERT OR REPLACE INTO discounts (id, name, code, type, value, minPurchase, maxDiscount, isActive)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """, (disc_id, name, code, disc_type, value, min_purchase, max_discount, is_active))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()

            return self.get_all()

    def _format_report_row(self, r):
        try:
            breakdown = json.loads(r.get("breakdown_json") or "[]")
        except Exception:
            breakdown = []
        return {
            "id": r.get("id", ""),
            "reportType": r.get("report_type", "daily"),
            "periodKey": r.get("period_key", ""),
            "label": r.get("label", ""),
            "startDate": r.get("start_date", ""),
            "endDate": r.get("end_date", ""),
            "totalTransactions": int(r.get("total_transactions", 0) or 0),
            "totalItems": int(r.get("total_items", 0) or 0),
            "totalRevenue": to_rupiah(r.get("total_revenue", 0)),
            "totalDiscount": to_rupiah(r.get("total_discount", 0)),
            "totalTax": to_rupiah(r.get("total_tax", 0)),
            "totalCost": to_rupiah(r.get("total_cost", 0)),
            "grossProfit": to_rupiah(r.get("gross_profit", 0)),
            "totalExpenses": to_rupiah(r.get("total_expenses", 0)),
            "netProfit": to_rupiah(r.get("net_profit", 0)),
            "breakdown": breakdown,
            "isConsolidated": bool(r.get("is_consolidated", 0)),
            "closedBy": r.get("closed_by") or "Otomatis (Tutup Toko)",
            "closedAt": r.get("closed_at") or ""
        }

    def _compute_day_metrics(self, target_date_key, all_trxs, all_exps):
        """Calculate exact revenue, HPP, gross profit, expenses, and net profit for a single YYYY-MM-DD."""
        day_trxs = [
            t for t in all_trxs
            if str(t.get("status") or "completed").lower() != "void"
            and to_local_date_key(t.get("date")) == target_date_key
        ]
        day_exps = [
            e for e in all_exps
            if to_local_date_key(e.get("date")) == target_date_key
        ]

        total_transactions = len(day_trxs)
        total_items = 0
        total_revenue = 0
        total_discount = 0
        total_tax = 0
        total_cost = 0
        gross_profit = 0
        payment_summary = {"cash": 0, "qris": 0, "transfer": 0}

        for t in day_trxs:
            items = t.get("items") or []
            total_items += sum(int(i.get("qty", 0) or 0) for i in items)
            rev = to_rupiah(t.get("total", 0))
            disc = to_rupiah(t.get("discount", 0))
            tax = to_rupiah(t.get("tax", 0))
            cost = to_rupiah(t.get("totalCost", 0))
            sub = to_rupiah(t.get("subtotal", rev))
            prof = to_rupiah(t.get("profit")) if t.get("profit") is not None else ((sub - disc) - cost)

            total_revenue += rev
            total_discount += disc
            total_tax += tax
            total_cost += cost
            gross_profit += prof

            pm = str(t.get("paymentMethod") or "cash").lower()
            if pm in payment_summary:
                payment_summary[pm] += rev
            else:
                payment_summary["cash"] += rev

        total_expenses = sum(to_rupiah(e.get("amount", 0)) for e in day_exps)
        net_profit = gross_profit - total_expenses

        expense_categories = {}
        for e in day_exps:
            cat = str(e.get("category") or "Operasional")
            expense_categories[cat] = expense_categories.get(cat, 0) + to_rupiah(e.get("amount", 0))

        breakdown = [{
            "type": "daily_detail",
            "date": target_date_key,
            "label": f"Harian: {format_id_date(target_date_key)}",
            "paymentSummary": payment_summary,
            "expenseCategories": expense_categories,
            "totalTransactions": total_transactions,
            "totalItems": total_items,
            "totalRevenue": total_revenue,
            "totalCost": total_cost,
            "grossProfit": gross_profit,
            "totalExpenses": total_expenses,
            "netProfit": net_profit
        }]

        return {
            "total_transactions": total_transactions,
            "total_items": total_items,
            "total_revenue": total_revenue,
            "total_discount": total_discount,
            "total_tax": total_tax,
            "total_cost": total_cost,
            "gross_profit": gross_profit,
            "total_expenses": total_expenses,
            "net_profit": net_profit,
            "breakdown_json": json.dumps(breakdown)
        }

    def _run_consolidation_sqlite(self, cur, closed_by="Otomatis (Konsolidasi)"):
        """
        Hierarchical Roll-up Engine (SQLite):
        - 7 Active Daily reports -> 1 Weekly report
        - 4 Active Weekly reports -> 1 Monthly report
        - 12 Active Monthly reports -> 1 Yearly report
        """
        now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()

        tiers = [
            ("daily", "weekly", 7, "RPT-WK", "Mingguan (7 Hari)"),
            ("weekly", "monthly", 4, "RPT-MO", "Bulanan (4 Minggu)"),
            ("monthly", "yearly", 12, "RPT-YR", "Tahunan (12 Bulan)")
        ]

        consolidated_events = []

        for src_type, dst_type, threshold, id_prefix, title_prefix in tiers:
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT id, report_type, period_key, label, start_date, end_date,
                       total_transactions, total_items, total_revenue, total_discount,
                       total_tax, total_cost, gross_profit, total_expenses, net_profit,
                       breakdown_json, is_consolidated, closed_by, closed_at
                FROM financial_reports
                WHERE report_type = ? AND COALESCE(is_consolidated, 0) = 0
                ORDER BY start_date ASC, created_at ASC
            """, (src_type,))
            active_rows = [dict(r) for r in cur.fetchall()]

            while len(active_rows) >= threshold:
                batch = active_rows[:threshold]
                active_rows = active_rows[threshold:]

                start_d = batch[0]["start_date"]
                end_d = batch[-1]["end_date"]
                new_id = generate_uid(id_prefix)
                period_key = f"{id_prefix}-{start_d}_{end_d}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4].upper()}"
                label = f"{title_prefix}: {format_id_date(start_d)} â€“ {format_id_date(end_d)}"

                sum_trx = sum(int(r.get("total_transactions", 0) or 0) for r in batch)
                sum_items = sum(int(r.get("total_items", 0) or 0) for r in batch)
                sum_rev = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_revenue", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_disc = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_discount", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_tax = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_tax", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_cost = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_cost", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_gross = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("gross_profit", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_exp = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_expenses", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_net = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("net_profit", 0)) for r in batch)

                children_summary = [self._format_report_row(r) for r in batch]
                for c in children_summary:
                    c.pop("breakdown", None)

                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO financial_reports (
                        id, report_type, period_key, label, start_date, end_date,
                        total_transactions, total_items, total_revenue, total_discount,
                        total_tax, total_cost, gross_profit, total_expenses, net_profit,
                        breakdown_json, is_consolidated, closed_by, closed_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    new_id, dst_type, period_key, label, start_d, end_d,
                    sum_trx, sum_items, sum_rev, sum_disc,
                    sum_tax, sum_cost, sum_gross, sum_exp, sum_net,
                    json.dumps(children_summary), closed_by, now_iso
                ))

                batch_ids = [r["id"] for r in batch]
                placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(batch_ids))
                cur.execute(f"UPDATE financial_reports SET is_consolidated = 1 WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", batch_ids)
                consolidated_events.append({"from": src_type, "to": dst_type, "label": label, "id": new_id})

        return consolidated_events

    def _run_consolidation_mysql(self, cur, closed_by="Otomatis (Konsolidasi)"):
        """
        Hierarchical Roll-up Engine (MySQL):
        - 7 Active Daily reports -> 1 Weekly report
        - 4 Active Weekly reports -> 1 Monthly report
        - 12 Active Monthly reports -> 1 Yearly report
        """
        now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()

        tiers = [
            ("daily", "weekly", 7, "RPT-WK", "Mingguan (7 Hari)"),
            ("weekly", "monthly", 4, "RPT-MO", "Bulanan (4 Minggu)"),
            ("monthly", "yearly", 12, "RPT-YR", "Tahunan (12 Bulan)")
        ]

        consolidated_events = []

        for src_type, dst_type, threshold, id_prefix, title_prefix in tiers:
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT id, report_type, period_key, label, start_date, end_date,
                       total_transactions, total_items,
                       CAST(total_revenue AS SIGNED) as total_revenue,
                       CAST(total_discount AS SIGNED) as total_discount,
                       CAST(total_tax AS SIGNED) as total_tax,
                       CAST(total_cost AS SIGNED) as total_cost,
                       CAST(gross_profit AS SIGNED) as gross_profit,
                       CAST(total_expenses AS SIGNED) as total_expenses,
                       CAST(net_profit AS SIGNED) as net_profit,
                       breakdown_json, is_consolidated, closed_by, closed_at
                FROM financial_reports
                WHERE report_type = %s AND COALESCE(is_consolidated, 0) = 0
                ORDER BY start_date ASC, created_at ASC
            """, (src_type,))
            active_rows = list(cur.fetchall())

            while len(active_rows) >= threshold:
                batch = active_rows[:threshold]
                active_rows = active_rows[threshold:]

                start_d = batch[0]["start_date"]
                end_d = batch[-1]["end_date"]
                new_id = generate_uid(id_prefix)
                period_key = f"{id_prefix}-{start_d}_{end_d}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4].upper()}"
                label = f"{title_prefix}: {format_id_date(start_d)} â€“ {format_id_date(end_d)}"

                sum_trx = sum(int(r.get("total_transactions", 0) or 0) for r in batch)
                sum_items = sum(int(r.get("total_items", 0) or 0) for r in batch)
                sum_rev = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_revenue", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_disc = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_discount", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_tax = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_tax", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_cost = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_cost", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_gross = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("gross_profit", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_exp = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("total_expenses", 0)) for r in batch)
                sum_net = sum(to_rupiah(r.get("net_profit", 0)) for r in batch)

                children_summary = [self._format_report_row(r) for r in batch]
                for c in children_summary:
                    c.pop("breakdown", None)

                cur.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO financial_reports (
                        id, report_type, period_key, label, start_date, end_date,
                        total_transactions, total_items, total_revenue, total_discount,
                        total_tax, total_cost, gross_profit, total_expenses, net_profit,
                        breakdown_json, is_consolidated, closed_by, closed_at
                    ) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, 0, %s, %s)
                """, (
                    new_id, dst_type, period_key, label, start_d, end_d,
                    sum_trx, sum_items, sum_rev, sum_disc,
                    sum_tax, sum_cost, sum_gross, sum_exp, sum_net,
                    json.dumps(children_summary), closed_by, now_iso
                ))

                batch_ids = [r["id"] for r in batch]
                placeholders = ",".join(["%s"] * len(batch_ids))
                cur.execute(f"UPDATE financial_reports SET is_consolidated = 1 WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", batch_ids)
                consolidated_events.append({"from": src_type, "to": dst_type, "label": label, "id": new_id})

        return consolidated_events

    def close_daily_report(self, target_date=None, closed_by="Otomatis (Tutup Toko)", force_new_entry=False):
        """
        Record or update the Daily Financial Report for `target_date` (YYYY-MM-DD),
        and automatically consolidate:
          - 7 Daily -> 1 Weekly
          - 4 Weekly -> 1 Monthly
          - 12 Monthly -> 1 Yearly
        """
        with self.lock:
            date_key = to_local_date_key(target_date) if target_date else datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
            state = self.get_all()
            all_trxs = state.get("transactions", [])
            all_exps = state.get("expenses", [])
            metrics = self._compute_day_metrics(date_key, all_trxs, all_exps)
            label = f"Harian: {format_id_date(date_key)}"

            consolidated_events = []

            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    conn.begin()
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("""
                            SELECT id, is_consolidated FROM financial_reports
                            WHERE report_type = 'daily' AND period_key = %s
                            ORDER BY is_consolidated ASC, created_at DESC LIMIT 1
                        """, (date_key,))
                        existing = cur.fetchone()

                        if existing and not int(existing.get("is_consolidated", 0)) and not force_new_entry:
                            rpt_id = existing["id"]
                            cur.execute("""
                                UPDATE financial_reports SET
                                    label = %s,
                                    total_transactions = %s,
                                    total_items = %s,
                                    total_revenue = %s,
                                    total_discount = %s,
                                    total_tax = %s,
                                    total_cost = %s,
                                    gross_profit = %s,
                                    total_expenses = %s,
                                    net_profit = %s,
                                    breakdown_json = %s,
                                    closed_by = %s,
                                    closed_at = %s
                                WHERE id = %s
                            """, (
                                label,
                                metrics["total_transactions"], metrics["total_items"],
                                metrics["total_revenue"], metrics["total_discount"],
                                metrics["total_tax"], metrics["total_cost"],
                                metrics["gross_profit"], metrics["total_expenses"],
                                metrics["net_profit"], metrics["breakdown_json"],
                                closed_by, now_iso, rpt_id
                            ))
                        else:
                            rpt_id = generate_uid("RPT-DAY")
                            p_key = date_key if not (existing and force_new_entry) else f"{date_key}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4].upper()}"
                            cur.execute("""
                                INSERT INTO financial_reports (
                                    id, report_type, period_key, label, start_date, end_date,
                                    total_transactions, total_items, total_revenue, total_discount,
                                    total_tax, total_cost, gross_profit, total_expenses, net_profit,
                                    breakdown_json, is_consolidated, closed_by, closed_at
                                ) VALUES (%s, 'daily', %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, 0, %s, %s)
                            """, (
                                rpt_id, p_key, label, date_key, date_key,
                                metrics["total_transactions"], metrics["total_items"],
                                metrics["total_revenue"], metrics["total_discount"],
                                metrics["total_tax"], metrics["total_cost"],
                                metrics["gross_profit"], metrics["total_expenses"],
                                metrics["net_profit"], metrics["breakdown_json"],
                                closed_by, now_iso
                            ))

                        consolidated_events = self._run_consolidation_mysql(cur, closed_by=closed_by)
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("""
                        SELECT id, is_consolidated FROM financial_reports
                        WHERE report_type = 'daily' AND period_key = ?
                        ORDER BY is_consolidated ASC, created_at DESC LIMIT 1
                    """, (date_key,))
                    row = cur.fetchone()
                    existing = dict(row) if row else None

                    if existing and not int(existing.get("is_consolidated", 0)) and not force_new_entry:
                        rpt_id = existing["id"]
                        cur.execute("""
                            UPDATE financial_reports SET
                                label = ?,
                                total_transactions = ?,
                                total_items = ?,
                                total_revenue = ?,
                                total_discount = ?,
                                total_tax = ?,
                                total_cost = ?,
                                gross_profit = ?,
                                total_expenses = ?,
                                net_profit = ?,
                                breakdown_json = ?,
                                closed_by = ?,
                                closed_at = ?
                            WHERE id = ?
                        """, (
                            label,
                            metrics["total_transactions"], metrics["total_items"],
                            metrics["total_revenue"], metrics["total_discount"],
                            metrics["total_tax"], metrics["total_cost"],
                            metrics["gross_profit"], metrics["total_expenses"],
                            metrics["net_profit"], metrics["breakdown_json"],
                            closed_by, now_iso, rpt_id
                        ))
                    else:
                        rpt_id = generate_uid("RPT-DAY")
                        p_key = date_key if not (existing and force_new_entry) else f"{date_key}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:4].upper()}"
                        cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO financial_reports (
                                id, report_type, period_key, label, start_date, end_date,
                                total_transactions, total_items, total_revenue, total_discount,
                                total_tax, total_cost, gross_profit, total_expenses, net_profit,
                                breakdown_json, is_consolidated, closed_by, closed_at
                            ) VALUES (?, 'daily', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)
                        """, (
                            rpt_id, p_key, label, date_key, date_key,
                            metrics["total_transactions"], metrics["total_items"],
                            metrics["total_revenue"], metrics["total_discount"],
                            metrics["total_tax"], metrics["total_cost"],
                            metrics["gross_profit"], metrics["total_expenses"],
                            metrics["net_profit"], metrics["breakdown_json"],
                            closed_by, now_iso
                        ))

                    consolidated_events = self._run_consolidation_sqlite(cur, closed_by=closed_by)
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()

            return {
                "closedDate": date_key,
                "consolidatedEvents": consolidated_events,
                "data": self.get_all()
            }

    def auto_check_and_close_reports(self):
        """
        Automatically close:
        1. Any past dates (< today) that have transactions or expenses but no daily report yet.
        2. Today's report if `autoCloseEnabled` is True and current local time >= `storeCloseTime`.
        Returns (changed: bool, full_data_or_none).
        """
        with self.lock:
            state = self.get_all()
            settings = state.get("settings", {})
            auto_enabled = settings.get("autoCloseEnabled", True)
            if str(auto_enabled).lower() == "false":
                return False, None

            close_time_str = str(settings.get("storeCloseTime") or "22:00").strip()
            now_dt = datetime.now()
            today_key = now_dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            now_hhmm = now_dt.strftime("%H:%M")

            existing_reports = state.get("financialReports", [])
            closed_daily_keys = {
                r["startDate"] for r in existing_reports
                if r.get("reportType") == "daily"
            }

            all_trxs = state.get("transactions", [])
            all_exps = state.get("expenses", [])

            candidate_dates = set()
            for t in all_trxs:
                if str(t.get("status") or "completed").lower() != "void":
                    dk = to_local_date_key(t.get("date"))
                    if dk < today_key and dk not in closed_daily_keys:
                        candidate_dates.add(dk)
            for e in all_exps:
                dk = to_local_date_key(e.get("date"))
                if dk < today_key and dk not in closed_daily_keys:
                    candidate_dates.add(dk)

            # Check if current time has reached storeCloseTime for today
            if len(close_time_str) == 5 and now_hhmm >= close_time_str:
                if today_key not in closed_daily_keys:
                    candidate_dates.add(today_key)

            if not candidate_dates:
                return False, None

            for dk in sorted(candidate_dates):
                self.close_daily_report(target_date=dk, closed_by="Otomatis (Jam Tutup Toko)")

            return True, self.get_all()

    def delete_financial_report(self, report_id):
        with self.lock:
            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM financial_reports WHERE id = %s", (report_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM financial_reports WHERE id = ?", (report_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            return self.get_all()

    def delete_discount(self, disc_id):
        with self.lock:
            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM discounts WHERE id = %s", (disc_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM discounts WHERE id = ?", (disc_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            return self.get_all()

    def toggle_discount(self, disc_id):
        with self.lock:
            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("UPDATE discounts SET isActive = CASE WHEN isActive = 1 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END WHERE id = %s", (disc_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("UPDATE discounts SET isActive = CASE WHEN isActive = 1 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END WHERE id = ?", (disc_id,))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            return self.get_all()

    def import_database(self, data):
        with self.lock:
            settings_data = data.get("settings") or {}
            categories_data = data.get("categories") or DEFAULT_CATEGORIES
            products_data = data.get("products") or []
            discounts_data = data.get("discounts") or []
            transactions_data = data.get("transactions") or []
            expenses_data = data.get("expenses") or []
            stock_history_data = data.get("stockHistory") or []

            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    conn.begin()
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM transaction_items")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM transactions")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM expenses")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM stock_history")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM discounts")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM financial_reports")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM daily_cashbook")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM products")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM categories")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM settings")

                        merged_settings = {**DEFAULT_SETTINGS, **settings_data}
                        for k, v in merged_settings.items():
                            cur.execute("INSERT INTO settings (setting_key, setting_val) VALUES (%s, %s)", (k, json.dumps(v)))

                        for cat in categories_data:
                            cur.execute("INSERT INTO categories (id, name, icon) VALUES (%s, %s, %s)", (cat.get("id", ""), cat.get("name", ""), cat.get("icon", "package")))

                        for p in products_data:
                            cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO products (id, name, barcode, category, costPrice, sellPrice, stock, minStock, unit, image, is_deleted)
                            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                            """, (p.get("id") or generate_uid("P"), p.get("name", ""), p.get("barcode", ""), p.get("category", "sembako"), p.get("costPrice", 0), p.get("sellPrice", 0), p.get("stock", 0), p.get("minStock", 5), p.get("unit", "pcs"), p.get("image", ""), 1 if p.get("is_deleted") else 0))

                        for d in discounts_data:
                            cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO discounts (id, name, code, type, value, minPurchase, maxDiscount, isActive)
                            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                            """, (d.get("id") or generate_uid("DSC"), d.get("name", ""), d.get("code", ""), d.get("type", "percentage"), d.get("value", 0), d.get("minPurchase", 0), d.get("maxDiscount", 0), 1 if d.get("isActive", True) else 0))

                        for t in transactions_data:
                            t_id = t.get("id") or generate_uid("TRX")
                            cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO transactions (id, date, cashier, customerName, subtotal, discount, tax, total, totalCost, profit, paymentMethod, amountPaid, changeAmount, status)
                            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                            """, (t_id, t.get("date", ""), t.get("cashier", "Kasir 1"), t.get("customerName", "Umum"), t.get("subtotal", 0), t.get("discount", 0), t.get("tax", 0), t.get("total", 0), t.get("totalCost", 0), t.get("profit", 0), t.get("paymentMethod", "cash"), t.get("amountPaid", 0), t.get("change", 0), t.get("status", "completed")))
                            for item in t.get("items", []):
                                cur.execute("""
                                INSERT INTO transaction_items (transaction_id, product_id, name, qty, costPrice, sellPrice, subtotal)
                                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                                """, (t_id, item.get("id", ""), item.get("name", ""), item.get("qty", 1), item.get("costPrice", 0), item.get("sellPrice", 0), item.get("subtotal", 0)))

                        for e in expenses_data:
                            cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO expenses (id, date, category, description, amount, recordedBy)
                            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                            """, (e.get("id") or generate_uid("EXP"), e.get("date", ""), e.get("category", "Operasional"), e.get("description", ""), e.get("amount", 0), e.get("recordedBy", "Admin")))

                        for s in stock_history_data:
                            cur.execute("""
                            INSERT INTO stock_history (id, date, productId, productName, type, qty, reason, user)
                            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s)
                            """, (s.get("id") or generate_uid("STK"), s.get("date", ""), s.get("productId", ""), s.get("productName", ""), s.get("type", "in"), s.get("qty", 0), s.get("reason", ""), s.get("user", "Admin")))
                    conn.commit()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM transaction_items")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM transactions")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM expenses")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM stock_history")

                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM categories")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM settings")

                    merged_settings = {**DEFAULT_SETTINGS, **settings_data}
                    for k, v in merged_settings.items():
                        cur.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (setting_key, setting_val) VALUES (?, ?)", (k, json.dumps(v)))

                    for cat in categories_data:
                        cur.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO categories (id, name, icon) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (cat.get("id", ""), cat.get("name", ""), cat.get("icon", "package")))

                    for p in products_data:

                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO products (id, name, barcode, category, costPrice, sellPrice, stock, minStock, unit, image, is_deleted)

                    for d in discounts_data:
                        cur.execute("""
                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO discounts (id, name, code, type, value, minPurchase, maxDiscount, isActive)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                        """, (d.get("id") or generate_uid("DSC"), d.get("name", ""), d.get("code", ""), d.get("type", "percentage"), d.get("value", 0), d.get("minPurchase", 0), d.get("maxDiscount", 0), 1 if d.get("isActive", True) else 0))

                    for t in transactions_data:
                        t_id = t.get("id") or generate_uid("TRX")
                        cur.execute("""
                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO transactions (id, date, cashier, customerName, subtotal, discount, tax, total, totalCost, profit, paymentMethod, amountPaid, changeAmount, status)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                        """, (t_id, t.get("date", ""), t.get("cashier", "Kasir 1"), t.get("customerName", "Umum"), t.get("subtotal", 0), t.get("discount", 0), t.get("tax", 0), t.get("total", 0), t.get("totalCost", 0), t.get("profit", 0), t.get("paymentMethod", "cash"), t.get("amountPaid", 0), t.get("change", 0), t.get("status", "completed")))
                        for item in t.get("items", []):

                            INSERT INTO transaction_items (transaction_id, product_id, name, qty, costPrice, sellPrice, subtotal)
                            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                            """, (t_id, item.get("id", ""), item.get("name", ""), item.get("qty", 1), item.get("costPrice", 0), item.get("sellPrice", 0), item.get("subtotal", 0)))

                finally:
                    conn.close()

            return self.get_all()

    def reset(self):
        with self.lock:
            if self.driver_type == "mysql":
                conn = self._get_mysql_connection()
                try:
                    conn.begin()
                    with conn.cursor() as cur:
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM transaction_items")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM transactions")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM expenses")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM stock_history")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM discounts")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM financial_reports")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM daily_cashbook")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM products")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM categories")
                        cur.execute("DELETE FROM settings")
                    conn.commit()
                    self._seed_if_empty_mysql()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            else:
                conn = self._get_sqlite_connection()
                try:
                    cur = conn.cursor()
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM transaction_items")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM transactions")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM expenses")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM stock_history")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM discounts")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM financial_reports")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM daily_cashbook")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM products")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM categories")
                    cur.execute("DELETE FROM settings")
                    conn.commit()
                    self._seed_if_empty_sqlite()
                except Exception:
                    conn.rollback()
                    raise
                finally:
                    conn.close()
            return self.get_all()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
WarungKu POS - Native ESC/POS 58mm & 80mm Thermal Printer Engine
Dioptimalkan khusus untuk VSC Desktop Thermal Printer Model: VSC TM-58D PRO
(58mm, USB+BT / GEZHI micro-printer VID_28E9&PID_0289, Power: 9V2A).
"""

import os
import time
import ctypes
from ctypes import wintypes
import subprocess

if os.name == "nt":
    import winreg


VIRTUAL_PRINTER_KEYWORDS = (
    "print to pdf",
    "xps document writer",
    "onenote",
    "fax",
    "send to",
    "adobe pdf",
    "foxit",
)

USBPRINT_GUID = "{28d78fad-5a12-11d1-ae5b-0000f803a8c2}"
GENERIC_WRITE = 0x40000000
FILE_SHARE_READ = 0x00000001
FILE_SHARE_WRITE = 0x00000002
OPEN_EXISTING = 3
INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE = ctypes.c_void_p(-1).value


def fmt_rp(val):
    try:
        n = int(round(float(val or 0)))
        return "Rp " + f"{n:,}".replace(",", ".")
    except Exception:
        return "Rp 0"


def lr_line(left_text, right_text, width=32):
    """Format left and right aligned text on a single thermal line (32 chars for 58mm)."""
    left_str = str(left_text or "")
    right_str = str(right_text or "")
    max_left = max(1, width - len(right_str) - 1)
    if len(left_str) > max_left:
        left_str = left_str[:max_left]
    spaces = max(1, width - len(left_str) - len(right_str))
    return left_str + (" " * spaces) + right_str


def build_escpos_receipt(trx, settings=None, paper_size="58mm"):
    """
    Build raw ESC/POS byte stream specifically calibrated for VSC TM-58D PRO (GEZHI VID_28E9&PID_0289).
    Uses standard POS-58 ESC ! commands, CR+LF (\r\n) line termination, and explicit heating density.
    """
    settings = settings or {}
    width = 48 if paper_size == "80mm" else 32
    sep = "-" * width
    double_sep = "=" * width

    store_name = str(settings.get("storeName") or "Warung Berkah Jaya").strip()
    store_addr = str(settings.get("storeAddress") or "").strip()
    store_phone = str(settings.get("storePhone") or "").strip()
    footer = str(settings.get("receiptFooter") or "Terima kasih atas kunjungan Anda!").strip()

    ESC = b"\x1b"

    # Standard POS-58 / VSC TM-58D PRO Initialization Sequence
    INIT = ESC + b"@"
    # Set Code Page 0 (PC437 Standard ASCII)
    CODEPAGE_PC437 = ESC + b"t\x00"
    # Set Line Spacing to default (ESC 2)
    LINE_SPACE_DEFAULT = ESC + b"2"
    # Alignment
    ALIGN_LEFT = ESC + b"a\x00"
    ALIGN_CENTER = ESC + b"a\x01"
    # Print modes via universal ESC ! n (supported by 100% of 58mm GEZHI/VSC printers)
    MODE_NORMAL = ESC + b"!\x00"
    MODE_BOLD = ESC + b"!\x08"
    MODE_HEADER = ESC + b"!\x18"  # Double height + Bold

    out = bytearray()
    out.extend(INIT)
    out.extend(CODEPAGE_PC437)
    out.extend(LINE_SPACE_DEFAULT)
    out.extend(MODE_NORMAL)

    def write_line(text=""):
        clean = (
            str(text)
            .replace("\u2022", "-")
            .replace("\u2014", "-")
            .replace("\u2013", "-")
            .encode("ascii", errors="replace")
        )
        # Use CR+LF (\r\n) so all GEZHI/POS-58 firmwares latch and print the line buffer
        out.extend(clean + b"\r\n")

    # Header (Centered)
    out.extend(ALIGN_CENTER)
    out.extend(MODE_HEADER)
    write_line(store_name.upper())
    out.extend(MODE_NORMAL)

    if store_addr:
        write_line(store_addr)
    if store_phone:
        write_line(f"Telp/WA: {store_phone}")
    write_line(double_sep)

    # Metadata (Left Aligned)
    out.extend(ALIGN_LEFT)
    trx_id = str(trx.get("id") or "-")
    trx_date = str(trx.get("date") or trx.get("createdAt") or "")
    if "T" in trx_date:
        trx_date = trx_date.replace("T", " ")[:16]
    cashier = str(trx.get("cashier") or trx.get("cashierName") or "Kasir")
    customer = str(trx.get("customerName") or "Umum")

    write_line(lr_line("No.Nota:", trx_id, width))
    write_line(lr_line("Waktu  :", trx_date, width))
    write_line(lr_line("Kasir  :", cashier, width))
    write_line(lr_line("Plg    :", customer, width))
    write_line(sep)

    # Items
    items = trx.get("items") or []
    for item in items:
        name = str(item.get("name") or "Produk")
        qty = int(item.get("qty") or 1)
        price = item.get("sellPrice") if item.get("sellPrice") is not None else (item.get("price") or 0)
        sub = item.get("subtotal") if item.get("subtotal") is not None else (qty * float(price))
        write_line(name[:width])
        write_line(lr_line(f"  {qty} x {fmt_rp(price)}", fmt_rp(sub), width))

    write_line(sep)

    # Totals
    subtotal = trx.get("subtotal") or 0
    discount = float(trx.get("discount") or 0)
    tax = float(trx.get("tax") or 0)
    total = trx.get("total") or 0
    amount_paid = trx.get("amountPaid") if trx.get("amountPaid") is not None else total
    change = trx.get("change") or 0
    method = str(trx.get("paymentMethod") or "TUNAI").upper()

    write_line(lr_line("Subtotal", fmt_rp(subtotal), width))
    if discount > 0:
        write_line(lr_line("Diskon", "-" + fmt_rp(discount), width))
    if tax > 0:
        write_line(lr_line("PPN", fmt_rp(tax), width))

    out.extend(MODE_BOLD)
    write_line(lr_line("TOTAL", fmt_rp(total), width))
    out.extend(MODE_NORMAL)

    write_line(lr_line(f"Bayar ({method})", fmt_rp(amount_paid), width))
    out.extend(MODE_BOLD)
    write_line(lr_line("Kembali", fmt_rp(change), width))
    out.extend(MODE_NORMAL)
    write_line(sep)

    # Footer
    out.extend(ALIGN_CENTER)
    for line in footer.splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            write_line(line.strip())

    # Feed 4 lines for manual tear bar on 58mm VSC TM-58D PRO
    out.extend(ALIGN_LEFT)
    out.extend(b"\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n")
    return bytes(out)


def _list_registry_usbprint_candidates():
    """Return candidate USBPRINT device paths registered in Windows without opening handles."""
    if os.name != "nt":
        return []
    candidates = []
    try:
        key_path = rf"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\DeviceClasses\{USBPRINT_GUID}"
        with winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key_path) as k:
            i = 0
            while True:
                try:
                    sub = winreg.EnumKey(k, i)
                    i += 1
                    if not sub.startswith("##?#"):
                        continue
                    dev_path = "\\\\?\\" + sub[4:]
                    label = "USB Direct: VSC TM-58D PRO (58mm)"
                    parts = sub.split("#")
                    if len(parts) >= 3:
                        hw_id = parts[2]
                        if "28E9" in hw_id.upper():
                            label = f"USB Direct: VSC TM-58D PRO ({hw_id})"
                        else:
                            label = f"USB Direct: Thermal Printer ({hw_id})"
                    candidates.append({
                        "label": label,
                        "devicePath": dev_path,
                    })
                except OSError:
                    break
    except Exception:
        pass
    return candidates


def find_active_usbprint_devices():
    """
    Scan Windows USB Printing Support (GUID_DEVINTERFACE_USBPRINT) interfaces
    and return all currently connected & writable USB thermal printers.
    """
    if os.name != "nt":
        return []

    active_devices = []
    try:
        kernel32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
        kernel32.CreateFileW.argtypes = [
            wintypes.LPCWSTR,
            wintypes.DWORD,
            wintypes.DWORD,
            wintypes.LPVOID,
            wintypes.DWORD,
            wintypes.DWORD,
            wintypes.HANDLE,
        ]
        kernel32.CreateFileW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
        kernel32.CloseHandle.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
        kernel32.CloseHandle.restype = wintypes.BOOL

        for cand in _list_registry_usbprint_candidates():
            h = kernel32.CreateFileW(
                cand["devicePath"],
                GENERIC_WRITE,
                FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
                None,
                OPEN_EXISTING,
                0,
                None,
            )
            if h != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE and h is not None:
                kernel32.CloseHandle(h)
                active_devices.append(cand)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return active_devices


def write_raw_to_usbprint_device(device_path, raw_bytes):
    """
    Write raw ESC/POS bytes to a Windows USBPRINT device interface path in 64-byte chunks
    (matching the 64-byte USB Full-Speed Bulk OUT endpoint of GEZHI VID_28E9&PID_0289).
    """
    kernel32 = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
    kernel32.CreateFileW.argtypes = [
        wintypes.LPCWSTR,
        wintypes.DWORD,
        wintypes.DWORD,
        wintypes.LPVOID,
        wintypes.DWORD,
        wintypes.DWORD,
        wintypes.HANDLE,
    ]
    kernel32.CreateFileW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
    kernel32.WriteFile.argtypes = [
        wintypes.HANDLE,
        wintypes.LPCVOID,
        wintypes.DWORD,
        ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD),
        wintypes.LPVOID,
    ]
    kernel32.WriteFile.restype = wintypes.BOOL
    kernel32.FlushFileBuffers.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    kernel32.FlushFileBuffers.restype = wintypes.BOOL
    kernel32.CloseHandle.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    kernel32.CloseHandle.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    h = kernel32.CreateFileW(
        device_path,
        GENERIC_WRITE,
        FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
        None,
        OPEN_EXISTING,
        0,
        None,
    )
    if h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE or h is None:
        err = ctypes.get_last_error()
        raise RuntimeError(f"Gagal membuka jalur USB printer '{device_path}' (Error code: {err}).")

    total_written = 0
    try:
        chunk_size = 64
        for offset in range(0, len(raw_bytes), chunk_size):
            chunk = raw_bytes[offset:offset + chunk_size]
            written = wintypes.DWORD(0)
            buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(chunk, len(chunk))
            ok = kernel32.WriteFile(h, buf, len(chunk), ctypes.byref(written), None)
            if not ok:
                err = ctypes.get_last_error()
                raise RuntimeError(f"Gagal mengirim data ke USB printer (Error code: {err}).")
            total_written += written.value
            time.sleep(0.008)

        kernel32.FlushFileBuffers(h)
        time.sleep(0.10)
        return {
            "success": True,
            "printerName": "VSC TM-58D PRO (USB Direct)",
            "devicePath": device_path,
            "bytesWritten": total_written,
        }
    finally:
        kernel32.CloseHandle(h)


def _is_virtual_printer(name):
    low = str(name or "").lower()
    return any(k in low for k in VIRTUAL_PRINTER_KEYWORDS)


class PRINTER_INFO_4W(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("pPrinterName", wintypes.LPWSTR),
        ("pServerName", wintypes.LPWSTR),
        ("Attributes", wintypes.DWORD),
    ]


def _enum_winspool_printer_names():
    """
    Fast, zero-subprocess Win32 EnumPrintersW call (< 1ms, no PowerShell cold-start lag).
    """
    if os.name != "nt":
        return []
    names = []
    try:
        PRINTER_ENUM_LOCAL = 0x00000002
        PRINTER_ENUM_CONNECTIONS = 0x00000004
        flags = PRINTER_ENUM_LOCAL | PRINTER_ENUM_CONNECTIONS

        winspool = ctypes.WinDLL("winspool.drv", use_last_error=True)
        winspool.EnumPrintersW.argtypes = [
            wintypes.DWORD,
            wintypes.LPWSTR,
            wintypes.DWORD,
            ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_byte),
            wintypes.DWORD,
            ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD),
            ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD),
        ]
        winspool.EnumPrintersW.restype = wintypes.BOOL

        needed = wintypes.DWORD(0)
        returned = wintypes.DWORD(0)
        winspool.EnumPrintersW(flags, None, 4, None, 0, ctypes.byref(needed), ctypes.byref(returned))
        if needed.value > 0:
            buf = (ctypes.c_byte * needed.value)()
            if winspool.EnumPrintersW(flags, None, 4, buf, needed.value, ctypes.byref(needed), ctypes.byref(returned)):
                info_array = ctypes.cast(buf, ctypes.POINTER(PRINTER_INFO_4W))
                for i in range(returned.value):
                    p_name = info_array[i].pPrinterName
                    if p_name and not _is_virtual_printer(p_name):
                        names.append(p_name.strip())
    except Exception:
        pass
    return names


def list_windows_printers():
    """
    Return list of available thermal printers:
    1. Active Direct USB Printing Support devices (VSC TM-58D PRO / USBPRINT)
    2. Physical Windows Spooler printers via fast native EnumPrintersW (excluding PDF/XPS/OneNote/Fax)
    """
    if os.name != "nt":
        return {"printers": [], "defaultPrinter": None, "recommendedPrinter": None}

    usb_devices = find_active_usbprint_devices()
    printers = [d["label"] for d in usb_devices]

    default_printer = None
    try:
        winspool = ctypes.WinDLL("winspool.drv", use_last_error=True)
        buf_size = wintypes.DWORD(260)
        buf = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(260)
        if winspool.GetDefaultPrinterW(buf, ctypes.byref(buf_size)):
            if not _is_virtual_printer(buf.value):
                default_printer = buf.value
    except Exception:
        pass

    spooler_printers = _enum_winspool_printer_names()

    thermal_keywords = ("vsc", "58", "pos", "tm-", "thermal", "receipt", "generic", "gezhi")

    def sort_key(p_name):
        low = p_name.lower()
        is_thermal = any(k in low for k in thermal_keywords)
        is_default = (p_name == default_printer)
        return (0 if is_thermal else 1, 0 if is_default else 1, p_name)

    spooler_printers.sort(key=sort_key)
    printers.extend(spooler_printers)

    recommended = printers[0] if printers else default_printer
    return {
        "printers": printers,
        "usbDevices": usb_devices,
        "defaultPrinter": default_printer,
        "recommendedPrinter": recommended,
    }


class DOC_INFO_1W(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [
        ("pDocName", wintypes.LPWSTR),
        ("pOutputFile", wintypes.LPWSTR),
        ("pDatatype", wintypes.LPWSTR),
    ]


def send_raw_to_windows_printer(printer_name, raw_bytes):
    """
    Send raw ESC/POS bytes to the VSC TM-58D PRO thermal printer.
    Opens the USB handle only ONCE per print job to avoid resetting USB endpoint state.
    """
    if os.name != "nt":
        raise RuntimeError("Fitur Direct Thermal Print hanya tersedia di Windows.")

    req_name = str(printer_name or "").strip()
    if _is_virtual_printer(req_name):
        req_name = ""

    # 1. Try Direct USB Thermal Printer candidates first without double-opening the handle
    candidates = _list_registry_usbprint_candidates()
    if candidates and (not req_name or req_name.lower().startswith("usb direct") or req_name.startswith("\\\\?\\")):
        last_err = None
        for cand in candidates:
            if req_name and req_name not in (cand["label"], cand["devicePath"]) and not req_name.lower().startswith("usb direct"):
                continue
            try:
                res = write_raw_to_usbprint_device(cand["devicePath"], raw_bytes)
                res["printerName"] = cand["label"]
                return res
            except Exception as e:
                last_err = e
        # If specific label didn't match, try all candidates
        for cand in candidates:
            try:
                res = write_raw_to_usbprint_device(cand["devicePath"], raw_bytes)
                res["printerName"] = cand["label"]
                return res
            except Exception as e:
                last_err = e

    # 2. Windows Spooler queue fallback
    if not req_name:
        info = list_windows_printers()
        req_name = info.get("recommendedPrinter") or ""

    if not req_name:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Printer VSC TM-58D PRO tidak terdeteksi di USB. Pastikan kabel USB printer terpasang ke PC dan tombol power printer menyala."
        )

    winspool = ctypes.WinDLL("winspool.drv", use_last_error=True)

    winspool.OpenPrinterW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPWSTR, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.HANDLE), wintypes.LPVOID]
    winspool.OpenPrinterW.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    winspool.StartDocPrinterW.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(DOC_INFO_1W)]
    winspool.StartDocPrinterW.restype = wintypes.DWORD

    winspool.StartPagePrinter.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    winspool.StartPagePrinter.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    winspool.WritePrinter.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.LPVOID, wintypes.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD)]
    winspool.WritePrinter.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    winspool.EndPagePrinter.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    winspool.EndPagePrinter.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    winspool.EndDocPrinter.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    winspool.EndDocPrinter.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    winspool.ClosePrinter.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    winspool.ClosePrinter.restype = wintypes.BOOL

    h_printer = wintypes.HANDLE()
    if not winspool.OpenPrinterW(req_name, ctypes.byref(h_printer), None):
        err = ctypes.get_last_error()
        raise RuntimeError(f"Gagal membuka printer '{req_name}' (Error code: {err}).")

    try:
        doc_info = DOC_INFO_1W()
        doc_info.pDocName = "WarungKu POS Thermal Receipt"
        doc_info.pOutputFile = None
        doc_info.pDatatype = "RAW"

        job_id = winspool.StartDocPrinterW(h_printer, 1, ctypes.byref(doc_info))
        if job_id == 0:
            err = ctypes.get_last_error()
            raise RuntimeError(f"StartDocPrinterW gagal pada '{req_name}' (Error code: {err}).")

        try:
            winspool.StartPagePrinter(h_printer)
            written = wintypes.DWORD(0)
            buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(raw_bytes)
            ok = winspool.WritePrinter(h_printer, buf, len(raw_bytes), ctypes.byref(written))
            winspool.EndPagePrinter(h_printer)
            if not ok:
                err = ctypes.get_last_error()
                raise RuntimeError(f"WritePrinter gagal (Error code: {err}).")
        finally:
            winspool.EndDocPrinter(h_printer)
    finally:
        winspool.ClosePrinter(h_printer)

    return {"success": True, "printerName": req_name, "bytesWritten": len(raw_bytes)}
