/**
 * WARUNGKU POS - CENTRAL STORE & REALTIME STATE MANAGEMENT
 * Terhubung langsung ke Backend SSE (/api/events) untuk sinkronisasi live
 * antara Kasir di HP dan Admin di PC.
 */

class AppStore {
  constructor() {
    this.authToken = localStorage.getItem('pos_auth_token') || null;
    this.isAuthenticated = Boolean(this.authToken);
    this.currentRole = localStorage.getItem('pos_role') || 'cashier'; // 'cashier' | 'admin'
    this.cashierName = localStorage.getItem('pos_cashier_name') || 'Kasir 1';
    this.isDark = localStorage.getItem('pos_theme') === 'dark';
    this.isLiveConnected = false;

    // Initial local cache or fallback seed data
    this.settings = this.loadData('pos_settings', DEFAULT_SETTINGS);
    this.categories = this.loadData('pos_categories', DEFAULT_CATEGORIES);
    this.products = this.loadData('pos_products', DEFAULT_PRODUCTS);
    this.transactions = this.loadData('pos_transactions', generateSampleTransactions());
    this.expenses = this.loadData('pos_expenses', DEFAULT_EXPENSES);
    this.stockHistory = this.loadData('pos_stock_history', DEFAULT_STOCK_HISTORY);
    this.discounts = this.loadData('pos_discounts', typeof DEFAULT_DISCOUNTS !== 'undefined' ? DEFAULT_DISCOUNTS : []);
    this.financialReports = this.loadData('pos_financial_reports', []);
    this.dailyCashbook = this.loadData('pos_daily_cashbook', []);

    // Active Cart for POS (kept locally per cashier session)
    this.cart = this.loadData('pos_active_cart', []);
    this.heldCarts = this.loadData('pos_held_carts', []);
    this.activeDiscount = Number(localStorage.getItem('pos_cart_discount') || 0);
    this.activeDiscountInfo = this.loadData('pos_cart_discount_info', null);
    this.customerName = localStorage.getItem('pos_customer_name') || 'Umum';
    this.pendingCheckouts = this.loadData('pos_pending_checkouts', []);
    this._isFlushingOfflineQueue = false;

    // Subscribed listeners
    this.listeners = [];

    // Apply dark mode on init
    this.applyTheme();

    // Automatically flush offline transactions when network reconnects
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      window.addEventListener('online', () => {
        if (this.isAuthenticated) {
          this.flushOfflineQueue();
        }
      });
    }

    // Start Realtime Connection to Server if authenticated
    if (this.isAuthenticated) {
      this.verifySessionAndConnect();
    }
  }

  async apiFetch(url, options = {}) {
    const headers = { ...(options.headers || {}) };
    if (this.authToken) {
      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${this.authToken}`;
    }
    const res = await fetch(url, { ...options, headers });
    if (res.status === 401 && !url.includes('/api/auth/login')) {
      this.clearAuthSession();
    }
    return res;
  }

  async verifySessionAndConnect() {
    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/auth/session');
      if (res.ok) {
        const sess = await res.json();
        this.currentRole = sess.role || this.currentRole;
        this.cashierName = sess.cashierName || this.cashierName;
        this.isAuthenticated = true;
        this.initRealtimeConnection();
        this.notify();
      } else if (res.status === 401) {
        this.clearAuthSession();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Offline fallback: allow cached session until server comes back
      this.initRealtimeConnection();
    }
  }

  async login(role, secret, cashierName = '') {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          role,
          password: secret,
          cashierName: cashierName || this.cashierName
        })
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok || !data.success) {
        return {
          success: false,
          message: data.error || 'Autentikasi gagal. Periksa kembali PIN / Password Anda.'
        };
      }

      this.authToken = data.token;
      this.isAuthenticated = true;
      this.currentRole = data.role;
      this.cashierName = data.cashierName || cashierName || (data.role === 'admin' ? 'Admin' : 'Kasir 1');

      localStorage.setItem('pos_auth_token', this.authToken);
      localStorage.setItem('pos_role', this.currentRole);
      localStorage.setItem('pos_cashier_name', this.cashierName);

      if (data.data) {
        this.applyServerState(data.data);
      }

      this.initRealtimeConnection();
      this.notify();
      return { success: true, role: this.currentRole, cashierName: this.cashierName };
    } catch (e) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Tidak dapat terhubung ke server autentikasi: ' + e.message
      };
    }
  }

  async logout() {
    try {
      if (this.authToken) {
        await this.apiFetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // ignore network error on logout
    }
    this.clearAuthSession();
  }

  clearAuthSession() {
    this.authToken = null;
    this.isAuthenticated = false;
    this.isLiveConnected = false;
    localStorage.removeItem('pos_auth_token');
    if (this.eventSource) {
      try { this.eventSource.close(); } catch (e) { }
      this.eventSource = null;
    }
    if (this._pollInterval) {
      clearInterval(this._pollInterval);
      this._pollInterval = null;
    }
    this.notify();
  }

  loadData(key, fallback) {
    try {
      const item = localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : fallback;
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`Error loading key ${key}:`, e);
      return fallback;
    }
  }

  saveData(key, data) {
    try {
      let toStore = data;
      // Cap large historical arrays in localStorage so browser 5MB quota is never exceeded
      if (Array.isArray(data) && (key === 'pos_transactions' || key === 'pos_stock_history' || key === 'pos_expenses')) {
        toStore = data.slice(0, 250);
      }
      localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(toStore));
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn(`Quota/Storage warning for key ${key}:`, e);
      try {
        if (Array.isArray(data)) {
          localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data.slice(0, 50)));
        }
      } catch (_) { }
    }
  }

  subscribe(listener) {
    this.listeners.push(listener);
    return () => {
      this.listeners = this.listeners.filter(l => l !== listener);
    };
  }

  notify() {
    this.listeners.forEach(fn => fn());
  }

  // ==================== REALTIME SSE ENGINE ====================
  initRealtimeConnection() {
    if (!this.authToken) return;

    if (this.eventSource) {
      try { this.eventSource.close(); } catch (e) { }
      this.eventSource = null;
    }

    if (typeof EventSource === 'undefined') {
      console.warn("Browser does not support SSE. Falling back to polling.");
      this.startPollingFallback();
      return;
    }

    try {
      // Authenticate via HttpOnly session cookie (warungku_session) so token never appears in URL logs
      this.eventSource = new EventSource('/api/events', { withCredentials: true });

      this.eventSource.onopen = () => {
        this.isLiveConnected = true;
        this.flushOfflineQueue();
        this.notify();
      };

      this.eventSource.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const payload = JSON.parse(event.data);
          this.handleServerEvent(payload);
        } catch (e) {
          // heartbeat or unparseable
        }
      };

      this.eventSource.onerror = () => {
        this.isLiveConnected = false;
        this.notify();
        // Browser automatically attempts reconnect
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("SSE connection error:", err);
      this.startPollingFallback();
    }
  }

  async flushOfflineQueue() {
    if (this._isFlushingOfflineQueue || !this.authToken) return;
    if (!Array.isArray(this.pendingCheckouts) || this.pendingCheckouts.length === 0) return;

    this._isFlushingOfflineQueue = true;
    try {
      const remaining = [];
      let syncedCount = 0;

      for (const pendingTrx of this.pendingCheckouts) {
        try {
          const payload = { ...pendingTrx };
          delete payload.pendingSync;
          const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/checkout', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload)
          });
          if (res.ok) {
            syncedCount++;
            const resJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            if (resJson.data) {
              this.applyServerState(resJson.data, true);
            }
          } else if (res.status >= 400 && res.status < 500) {
            // Validation rejected by server (e.g. duplicate or invalid): remove from queue so it doesn't block forever
            console.warn('Offline transaction rejected by server validation:', pendingTrx.id);
          } else {
            remaining.push(pendingTrx);
          }
        } catch (netErr) {
          // Still offline
          remaining.push(pendingTrx);
        }
      }

      this.pendingCheckouts = remaining;
      this.saveData('pos_pending_checkouts', this.pendingCheckouts);

      if (syncedCount > 0) {
        this.showRealtimeToast(`â˜ï¸ ${syncedCount} transaksi offline berhasil disinkronkan ke server!`);
        await this.fetchServerData();
      }
    } finally {
      this._isFlushingOfflineQueue = false;
    }
  }

  startPollingFallback() {
    if (this._pollInterval) clearInterval(this._pollInterval);
    this._pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
      if (this.authToken) this.fetchServerData();
    }, 4000);
  }

  async fetchServerData() {
    if (!this.authToken) return;
    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/data');
      if (res.ok) {
        const fullData = await res.json();
        this.applyServerState(fullData);
        this.isLiveConnected = true;
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      this.isLiveConnected = false;
    }
  }

  handleServerEvent(event) {
    if (!event || !event.type) return;

    if (event.type === 'init' && event.data) {
      this.applyServerState(event.data);
      this.notify();
      return;
    }

    if (event.data && event.data.fullData) {
      this.applyServerState(event.data.fullData);

      // Realtime Notification for Admin when a new checkout occurs
      if (event.type === 'checkout' && event.data.transaction) {
        const trx = event.data.transaction;
        this.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ”” Transaksi Baru: ${formatRupiah(trx.total)} oleh ${trx.cashier}`);
        if (this.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') {
          soundFx.playSuccess();
        }
      }

      this.notify();
    }
  }

  applyServerState(serverData, skipFlush = false) {
    if (serverData.products) {
      this.products = serverData.products;
      this.saveData('pos_products', this.products);
    }
    if (serverData.transactions) {
      // Preserve any unsynced offline transactions so they are NEVER overwritten by SSE state updates
      const serverIds = new Set(serverData.transactions.map(t => t.id));
      this.pendingCheckouts = (this.pendingCheckouts || []).filter(pt => !serverIds.has(pt.id));
      this.saveData('pos_pending_checkouts', this.pendingCheckouts);

      this.transactions = [
        ...this.pendingCheckouts,
        ...serverData.transactions
      ];
      this.saveData('pos_transactions', this.transactions);

      if (!skipFlush && this.pendingCheckouts.length > 0) {
        setTimeout(() => this.flushOfflineQueue(), 100);
      }
    }
    if (serverData.expenses) {
      this.expenses = serverData.expenses;
      this.saveData('pos_expenses', this.expenses);
    }
    if (serverData.stockHistory) {
      this.stockHistory = serverData.stockHistory;
      this.saveData('pos_stock_history', this.stockHistory);
    }
    if (serverData.discounts) {
      this.discounts = serverData.discounts;
      this.saveData('pos_discounts', this.discounts);
    }
    if (serverData.settings) {
      this.settings = { ...this.settings, ...serverData.settings };
      this.saveData('pos_settings', this.settings);
    }
    if (serverData.categories) {
      this.categories = serverData.categories;
      this.saveData('pos_categories', this.categories);
    }
    if (serverData.financialReports) {
      this.financialReports = serverData.financialReports;
      this.saveData('pos_financial_reports', this.financialReports);
    }
    if (serverData.dailyCashbook) {
      this.dailyCashbook = serverData.dailyCashbook;
      this.saveData('pos_daily_cashbook', this.dailyCashbook);
    }
  }

  showRealtimeToast(message) {
    let toast = document.getElementById('pos-live-toast');
    if (!toast) {
      toast = document.createElement('div');
      toast.id = 'pos-live-toast';
      toast.className = 'fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 text-white shadow-2xl border border-emerald-500/40 text-xs font-bold flex items-center space-x-2 animate-in slide-in-from-top duration-200';
      document.body.appendChild(toast);
    }
    toast.innerHTML = `<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping mr-1"></span> ${message}`;
    toast.style.display = 'flex';

    if (this._toastTimer) clearTimeout(this._toastTimer);
    this._toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      toast.style.display = 'none';
    }, 4500);
  }

  // ==================== ROLE & THEME ====================
  setRole(role) {
    this.currentRole = role;
    localStorage.setItem('pos_role', role);
    this.notify();
  }

  setCashierName(name) {
    this.cashierName = name;
    localStorage.setItem('pos_cashier_name', name);
    this.notify();
  }

  toggleTheme() {
    this.isDark = !this.isDark;
    localStorage.setItem('pos_theme', this.isDark ? 'dark' : 'light');
    this.applyTheme();
    this.notify();
  }

  applyTheme() {
    if (this.isDark) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
    }
  }

  // ==================== CART OPERATIONS ====================
  addToCart(productId, qtyToAdd = 1) {
    const product = this.products.find(p => p.id === productId);
    if (!product) return { success: false, message: 'Produk tidak ditemukan' };

    const cartItem = this.cart.find(item => item.id === productId);
    const currentQtyInCart = cartItem ? cartItem.qty : 0;

    if (currentQtyInCart + qtyToAdd > product.stock) {
      if (this.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playError();
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Stok tidak mencukupi! Sisa stok terkini: ${product.stock}`
      };
    }

    if (cartItem) {
      cartItem.qty += qtyToAdd;
      cartItem.subtotal = cartItem.qty * cartItem.sellPrice;
    } else {
      this.cart.push({
        id: product.id,
        name: product.name,
        barcode: product.barcode,
        costPrice: product.costPrice,
        sellPrice: product.sellPrice,
        unit: product.unit,
        qty: qtyToAdd,
        subtotal: product.sellPrice * qtyToAdd,
        image: product.image
      });
    }

    if (this.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playBeep();
    this.persistCart();
    this.notify();
    return { success: true };
  }

  updateCartQty(productId, newQty) {
    const product = this.products.find(p => p.id === productId);
    const cartIndex = this.cart.findIndex(item => item.id === productId);

    if (cartIndex === -1) return;

    if (newQty <= 0) {
      this.cart.splice(cartIndex, 1);
    } else {
      if (product && newQty > product.stock) {
        if (this.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playError();
        alert(`Stok hanya tersedia ${product.stock} ${product.unit}`);
        return;
      }
      this.cart[cartIndex].qty = newQty;
      this.cart[cartIndex].subtotal = newQty * this.cart[cartIndex].sellPrice;
    }

    this.persistCart();
    this.notify();
  }

  removeFromCart(productId) {
    this.cart = this.cart.filter(item => item.id !== productId);
    this.persistCart();
    this.notify();
  }

  clearCart() {
    this.cart = [];
    this.activeDiscount = 0;
    this.activeDiscountInfo = null;
    this.customerName = 'Umum';
    localStorage.removeItem('pos_cart_discount');
    localStorage.removeItem('pos_cart_discount_info');
    localStorage.removeItem('pos_customer_name');
    this.persistCart();
    this.notify();
  }

  setDiscount(amount, info = null) {
    this.activeDiscount = Math.max(0, Number(amount) || 0);
    this.activeDiscountInfo = info || (this.activeDiscount > 0 ? { name: 'Diskon Manual', type: 'fixed', value: this.activeDiscount } : null);
    localStorage.setItem('pos_cart_discount', this.activeDiscount);
    if (this.activeDiscountInfo) {
      localStorage.setItem('pos_cart_discount_info', JSON.stringify(this.activeDiscountInfo));
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('pos_cart_discount_info');
    }
    this.notify();
  }

  applyDiscountPromo(discountOrCode) {
    const subtotal = this.cart.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.subtotal, 0);
    if (subtotal <= 0) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Keranjang belanja masih kosong!' };
    }

    let discount = null;
    if (typeof discountOrCode === 'object' && discountOrCode !== null) {
      discount = discountOrCode;
    } else {
      const query = String(discountOrCode || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      discount = this.discounts.find(d =>
        (d.code && d.code.toUpperCase() === query) || d.id === discountOrCode || d.name.toUpperCase() === query
      );
    }

    if (!discount) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Kode promo / voucher diskon tidak ditemukan!' };
    }

    if (!discount.isActive) {
      return { success: false, message: `Promo "${discount.name}" sedang tidak aktif!` };
    }

    if (discount.minPurchase && subtotal < Number(discount.minPurchase)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Minimal belanja untuk promo ini adalah ${formatRupiah(discount.minPurchase)} (Saat ini: ${formatRupiah(subtotal)})`
      };
    }

    let calculatedDiscount = 0;
    if (discount.type === 'percentage') {
      calculatedDiscount = Math.round((subtotal * Number(discount.value)) / 100);
      if (discount.maxDiscount && Number(discount.maxDiscount) > 0) {
        calculatedDiscount = Math.min(calculatedDiscount, Number(discount.maxDiscount));
      }
    } else {
      calculatedDiscount = Math.min(Number(discount.value) || 0, subtotal);
    }

    this.setDiscount(calculatedDiscount, {
      id: discount.id,
      name: discount.name,
      code: discount.code || '',
      type: discount.type,
      value: Number(discount.value),
      maxDiscount: Number(discount.maxDiscount || 0),
      minPurchase: Number(discount.minPurchase || 0)
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      discountAmount: calculatedDiscount,
      name: discount.name,
      code: discount.code
    };
  }

  removeDiscount() {
    this.setDiscount(0, null);
  }

  setCustomerName(name) {
    this.customerName = name || 'Umum';
    localStorage.setItem('pos_customer_name', this.customerName);
    this.notify();
  }

  holdCart(note = '') {
    if (this.cart.length === 0) return false;
    const held = {
      id: 'HOLD-' + Date.now(),
      date: new Date().toISOString(),
      customerName: this.customerName,
      note: note || `Pesanan ${this.customerName}`,
      cart: [...this.cart],
      discount: this.activeDiscount,
      discountInfo: this.activeDiscountInfo
    };
    this.heldCarts.push(held);
    this.saveData('pos_held_carts', this.heldCarts);
    this.clearCart();
    return true;
  }

  restoreHeldCart(holdId) {
    const index = this.heldCarts.findIndex(h => h.id === holdId);
    if (index === -1) return;
    const held = this.heldCarts.splice(index, 1)[0];

    // If current cart is not empty, preserve it as a held cart so items aren't lost
    if (this.cart.length > 0) {
      this.heldCarts.push({
        id: 'HOLD-' + Date.now(),
        date: new Date().toISOString(),
        customerName: this.customerName,
        note: `Pesanan ${this.customerName} (Disimpan Otomatis)`,
        cart: [...this.cart],
        discount: this.activeDiscount,
        discountInfo: this.activeDiscountInfo
      });
    }

    this.cart = held.cart || [];
    this.activeDiscount = Number(held.discount || 0);
    this.activeDiscountInfo = held.discountInfo || null;
    this.customerName = held.customerName || 'Umum';

    localStorage.setItem('pos_cart_discount', this.activeDiscount);
    if (this.activeDiscountInfo) {
      localStorage.setItem('pos_cart_discount_info', JSON.stringify(this.activeDiscountInfo));
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('pos_cart_discount_info');
    }
    localStorage.setItem('pos_customer_name', this.customerName);

    this.saveData('pos_held_carts', this.heldCarts);
    this.persistCart();
    this.notify();
  }

  deleteHeldCart(holdId) {
    this.heldCarts = this.heldCarts.filter(h => h.id !== holdId);
    this.saveData('pos_held_carts', this.heldCarts);
    this.notify();
  }

  persistCart() {
    this.saveData('pos_active_cart', this.cart);
  }

  getCartCalculations() {
    const subtotal = this.cart.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.subtotal, 0);
    const totalCost = this.cart.reduce((acc, item) => acc + (item.costPrice * item.qty), 0);

    // Dynamic recalculation for percentage discount if cart contents changed
    let effectiveDiscount = this.activeDiscount;
    if (this.activeDiscountInfo && this.activeDiscountInfo.type === 'percentage') {
      if (this.activeDiscountInfo.minPurchase && subtotal < this.activeDiscountInfo.minPurchase) {
        effectiveDiscount = 0;
      } else {
        let disc = Math.round((subtotal * Number(this.activeDiscountInfo.value)) / 100);
        if (this.activeDiscountInfo.maxDiscount && Number(this.activeDiscountInfo.maxDiscount) > 0) {
          disc = Math.min(disc, Number(this.activeDiscountInfo.maxDiscount));
        }
        effectiveDiscount = disc;
      }
    }

    const discount = Math.min(effectiveDiscount, subtotal);
    const taxableAmount = Math.max(0, subtotal - discount);
    const tax = this.settings.taxRate > 0 ? Math.round((taxableAmount * this.settings.taxRate) / 100) : 0;
    const total = taxableAmount + tax;
    const profit = (subtotal - discount) - totalCost;
    const totalItems = this.cart.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.qty, 0);

    return {
      subtotal,
      totalCost,
      discount,
      tax,
      total,
      profit,
      totalItems,
      discountInfo: this.activeDiscountInfo
    };
  }

  // ==================== CHECKOUT (POST TO SERVER) ====================
  async processCheckout(paymentMethod, amountPaid = 0, customerName = 'Umum') {
    if (this.cart.length === 0) return null;

    const calc = this.getCartCalculations();
    const finalAmountPaid = paymentMethod === 'cash' ? Math.max(amountPaid, calc.total) : calc.total;
    const change = Math.max(0, finalAmountPaid - calc.total);

    const randHex = Array.from({ length: 6 }, () => Math.floor(Math.random() * 16).toString(16)).join('').toUpperCase();
    const transactionId = 'TRX-' + Date.now() + '-' + randHex;

    const newTransaction = {
      id: transactionId,
      date: new Date().toISOString(),
      cashier: this.cashierName,
      customerName: customerName || this.customerName,
      items: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(this.cart)),
      subtotal: calc.subtotal,
      discount: calc.discount,
      discountInfo: this.activeDiscountInfo,
      adminAuthPassword: this.activeDiscountInfo?.adminAuthPassword || undefined,
      tax: calc.tax,
      total: calc.total,
      totalCost: calc.totalCost,
      profit: calc.profit,
      paymentMethod,
      amountPaid: finalAmountPaid,
      change,
      status: 'completed'
    };

    // Broadcast to Server via authenticated REST API first so server validates stock & prices
    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/checkout', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(newTransaction)
      });
      if (!res.ok) {
        const errJson = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (this.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') {
          soundFx.playError();
        }
        alert(errJson.error || 'Gagal memproses transaksi di server.');
        await this.fetchServerData();
        return null;
      }
      const resJson = await res.json();
      if (resJson.transaction) {
        Object.assign(newTransaction, resJson.transaction);
      }
      if (resJson.data) {
        this.applyServerState(resJson.data);
      }
      this.clearCart();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Offline checkout queued for sync:", e);
      // Queue offline transaction in persistent pos_pending_checkouts so it syncs when Wi-Fi returns
      newTransaction.pendingSync = true;
      this.pendingCheckouts = [newTransaction, ...(this.pendingCheckouts || [])];
      this.saveData('pos_pending_checkouts', this.pendingCheckouts);

      this.cart.forEach(item => {
        const prod = this.products.find(p => p.id === item.id);
        if (prod) {
          prod.stock = Math.max(0, prod.stock - item.qty);
        }
      });
      this.transactions.unshift(newTransaction);
      this.saveData('pos_products', this.products);
      this.saveData('pos_transactions', this.transactions);
      this.clearCart();
      this.showRealtimeToast('ðŸ“¡ Offline: Transaksi disimpan ke antrean sinkronisasi & akan dikirim otomatis saat jaringan kembali.');
    }

    if (this.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') {
      soundFx.playSuccess();
    }

    this.notify();
    return newTransaction;
  }

  async voidTransaction(trxId, reason = 'Dibatalkan oleh Admin') {
    const trx = this.transactions.find(t => t.id === trxId);
    if (!trx || trx.status === 'void') return;

    // Optimistically mark status as void & restore stock locally
    trx.status = 'void';
    if (Array.isArray(trx.items)) {
      trx.items.forEach(item => {
        const prod = this.products.find(p => p.id === item.id);
        if (prod) prod.stock = Number(prod.stock || 0) + Number(item.qty || 0);
      });
    }
    this.saveData('pos_products', this.products);
    this.saveData('pos_transactions', this.transactions);

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/transactions/void', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: trxId, reason })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Offline transaction void:", e);
    }
    this.notify();
  }

  async deleteTransaction(trxId, reason = 'Dibatalkan oleh Admin') {
    return this.voidTransaction(trxId, reason);
  }

  // ==================== PRODUCT CRUD (SYNCED) ====================
  async saveProduct(productData) {
    let productId = productData.id || ('P' + String(Date.now()).slice(-6));

    const finalProduct = {
      id: productId,
      name: productData.name,
      barcode: productData.barcode || String(Math.floor(10000000 + Math.random() * 90000000)),
      category: productData.category || 'lainnya',
      costPrice: Math.round(Number(productData.costPrice) || 0),
      sellPrice: Math.round(Number(productData.sellPrice) || 0),
      stock: Number(productData.stock) || 0,
      minStock: Number(productData.minStock) || 5,
      unit: productData.unit || 'pcs',
      image: productData.image || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1542838132-92c53300491e?w=400&auto=format&fit=crop&q=60'
    };

    // Optimistic local update
    const existingIdx = this.products.findIndex(p => p.id === productId);
    if (existingIdx >= 0) {
      this.products[existingIdx] = finalProduct;
    } else {
      this.products.push(finalProduct);
    }
    this.saveData('pos_products', this.products);
    this.notify();

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/products', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(finalProduct)
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Server offline, saved locally:", e);
    }

    return productId;
  }

  async deleteProduct(productId) {
    this.products = this.products.filter(p => p.id !== productId);
    this.saveData('pos_products', this.products);
    this.notify();

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch(`/api/products?id=${encodeURIComponent(productId)}`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Server offline, deleted locally:", e);
    }
  }

  // Stock In / Stock Out Mutation (Restock)
  async adjustStock(productId, qty, type, reason = '', recordExpense = false) {
    const numQty = Math.abs(Number(qty) || 0);
    const payload = {
      productId,
      qty: numQty,
      type,
      reason,
      recordExpense,
      user: 'Admin'
    };

    // Optimistic local update
    const prod = this.products.find(p => p.id === productId);
    if (prod) {
      prod.stock = type === 'in' ? Number(prod.stock) + numQty : Math.max(0, Number(prod.stock) - numQty);
      this.saveData('pos_products', this.products);
    }
    this.notify();

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/stock', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Stock mutation error:", e);
    }
  }

  // ==================== DISCOUNT & PROMO CRUD (SYNCED) ====================
  async saveDiscount(discountData) {
    let discountId = discountData.id || ('DSC-' + String(Date.now()).slice(-6));

    const finalDiscount = {
      id: discountId,
      name: discountData.name,
      code: (discountData.code || '').toUpperCase().trim(),
      type: discountData.type || 'percentage',
      value: Math.round(Number(discountData.value) || 0),
      minPurchase: Math.round(Number(discountData.minPurchase) || 0),
      maxDiscount: Math.round(Number(discountData.maxDiscount) || 0),
      isActive: discountData.isActive !== undefined ? Boolean(discountData.isActive) : true
    };

    const existingIdx = this.discounts.findIndex(d => d.id === discountId);
    if (existingIdx >= 0) {
      this.discounts[existingIdx] = finalDiscount;
    } else {
      this.discounts.unshift(finalDiscount);
    }
    this.saveData('pos_discounts', this.discounts);
    this.notify();

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/discounts', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...finalDiscount, isActive: finalDiscount.isActive ? 1 : 0 })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Server error, discount saved locally:", e);
    }

    return finalDiscount;
  }

  async deleteDiscount(discountId) {
    this.discounts = this.discounts.filter(d => d.id !== discountId);
    this.saveData('pos_discounts', this.discounts);
    this.notify();

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch(`/api/discounts?id=${encodeURIComponent(discountId)}`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Server error, discount deleted locally:", e);
    }
  }

  async toggleDiscount(discountId) {
    const disc = this.discounts.find(d => d.id === discountId);
    if (disc) {
      disc.isActive = !disc.isActive;
      this.saveData('pos_discounts', this.discounts);
      this.notify();
    }

    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/discounts/toggle', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: discountId })
      });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
        this.notify();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("Server error, discount toggle failed locally:", e);
    }
  }

    }
  }

  exportBackupJSON() {
    const backup = {
      version: "2.0-realtime",
      exportDate: new Date().toISOString(),
      settings: this.settings,
      categories: this.categories,
      products: this.products,
      transactions: this.transactions,
      expenses: this.expenses,
      stockHistory: this.stockHistory,
      discounts: this.discounts,
      dailyCashbook: this.dailyCashbook,
      financialReports: this.financialReports
    };
    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(backup, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `backup_warung_realtime_${getLocalDateKey(new Date())}.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  async importBackupJSON(jsonData) {
    try {
      const data = typeof jsonData === 'string' ? JSON.parse(jsonData) : jsonData;
      if (data.products && data.settings) {
        const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/import', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(data)
        });
        if (res.ok) {
          const json = await res.json();
          if (json.data) this.applyServerState(json.data);
          this.notify();
          return { success: true, message: 'Database toko berhasil dipulihkan di server!' };
        }
      }
      return { success: false, message: 'Format backup tidak valid.' };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Gagal memulihkan data: ' + e.message };
    }
  }

  async resetToDefault() {
    try {
      await this.apiFetch('/api/reset', { method: 'POST' });
      const token = this.authToken;
      const role = this.currentRole;
      const cashierName = this.cashierName;
      localStorage.clear();
      if (token) localStorage.setItem('pos_auth_token', token);
      if (role) localStorage.setItem('pos_role', role);
      if (cashierName) localStorage.setItem('pos_cashier_name', cashierName);
      window.location.reload();
    } catch (e) {
      window.location.reload();
    }
  }

  async closeStoreDay(targetDate = null, forceNew = false) {
    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch('/api/reports/close-day', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          date: targetDate,
          closedBy: `${this.cashierName || 'Admin'} (Tutup Toko)`,
          forceNew
        })
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, message: data.error || 'Gagal memproses tutup buku toko.' };
      }
      if (data.data) {
        this.applyServerState(data.data);
      }
      this.notify();
      return {
        success: true,
        closedDate: data.closedDate,
        consolidatedEvents: data.consolidatedEvents || []
      };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, message: e.message };
    }
  }

  async deleteFinancialReport(reportId) {
    try {
      const res = await this.apiFetch(`/api/reports?id=${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`, {
        method: 'DELETE'
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok) {
        return { success: false, message: data.error || 'Gagal menghapus laporan.' };
      }
      if (data.data) {
        this.applyServerState(data.data);
      }
      this.notify();
      return { success: true };
    } catch (e) {
      return { success: false, message: e.message };
    }
  }
}

// Helpers for formatting & local timezone dates
function getLocalDateKey(dateInput) {
  if (!dateInput) return '';
  const d = dateInput instanceof Date ? dateInput : new Date(dateInput);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return String(dateInput).split('T')[0];
  const year = d.getFullYear();
  const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

function escapeHtml(str) {
  if (str === null || str === undefined) return '';
  return String(str)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

function formatRupiah(number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('id-ID', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'IDR',
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 0
  }).format(number || 0);
}

function formatDate(dateString) {
  if (!dateString) return '-';
  const d = new Date(dateString);
  return d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric'
  });
}

function formatTime(dateString) {
  if (!dateString) return '-';
  const d = new Date(dateString);
  return d.toLocaleTimeString('id-ID', {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit'
  });
}

function formatDateTime(dateString) {
  if (!dateString) return '-';
  return `${formatDate(dateString)}, ${formatTime(dateString)}`;
}

// Global Store Instance

/**
 * WARUNGKU POS - BUKU KAS HARIAN (OPERATIONAL CASHBOOK COMPONENT)
 * Mengelola arus pergerakan uang riil kas toko secara kronologis per hari:
 * - Pemilihan tanggal (Date navigation: kalender, Hari Ini, Prev/Next)
 * - Saldo Awal, Total Masuk (Penjualan + Modal Lain), Total Keluar (Belanja Stok + Beban Operasional), Saldo Akhir
 * - Rincian kanal pembayaran: Kas Tunai Laci, Rekening Bank, QRIS/E-Wallet
 * - Pencatatan Uang Masuk (Modal Tambahan, dll) & Uang Keluar (Beban operasional/belanja)
 * - Anti-duplikasi: Terkoneksi otomatis dengan transaksi penjualan kasir (reference = trx.id)
 */

let cashbookSelectedDate = getLocalDateKey(new Date());
let cashbookTypeFilter = 'all'; // 'all' | 'in' | 'out'
let cashbookMethodFilter = 'all'; // 'all' | 'cash' | 'bank' | 'qris'
let cashbookSearchQuery = '';

function setCashbookDate(newDateStr) {
  if (!newDateStr) return;
  cashbookSelectedDate = newDateStr;
  window.renderApp();
}

function shiftCashbookDate(deltaDays) {
  try {
    const parts = (cashbookSelectedDate || getLocalDateKey(new Date())).split('-');
    const curDate = new Date(Number(parts[0]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[2]));
    curDate.setDate(curDate.getDate() + deltaDays);
    cashbookSelectedDate = getLocalDateKey(curDate);
    window.renderApp();
  } catch (e) {
    cashbookSelectedDate = getLocalDateKey(new Date());
    window.renderApp();
  }
}

function renderAdminCashbook() {
  const store = window.store;
  const targetDateKey = cashbookSelectedDate || getLocalDateKey(new Date());

  // Format tanggal ramah bahasa Indonesia
  let dateDisplay = targetDateKey;
  try {
    const parts = targetDateKey.split('-');
    const d = new Date(Number(parts[0]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[2]));
    dateDisplay = d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'long', year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
  } catch (e) {}

  const allCashEntries = store.dailyCashbook || [];

  // 1. Hitung Saldo Awal: akumulasi seluruh mutasi kas aktif sebelum tanggal terpilih
  const priorActiveEntries = allCashEntries.filter(e => {
    if (e.status === 'void') return false;
    const entryDateKey = getLocalDateKey(e.date);
    return entryDateKey < targetDateKey;
  });

  const priorIn = priorActiveEntries.filter(e => e.type === 'in').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const priorOut = priorActiveEntries.filter(e => e.type === 'out').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const openingBalance = Math.max(0, priorIn - priorOut);

  // 2. Data mutasi kas pada hari terpilih
  const currentDayEntries = allCashEntries.filter(e => {
    const entryDateKey = getLocalDateKey(e.date);
    return entryDateKey === targetDateKey;
  });

  // Filter tampilan berdasarkan tipe, metode, dan pencarian
  const filteredEntries = currentDayEntries.filter(e => {
    const matchType = cashbookTypeFilter === 'all' || e.type === cashbookTypeFilter;
    let matchMethod = true;
    if (cashbookMethodFilter === 'cash') matchMethod = e.method === 'cash';
    else if (cashbookMethodFilter === 'bank') matchMethod = e.method === 'bank' || e.method === 'transfer';
    else if (cashbookMethodFilter === 'qris') matchMethod = e.method === 'qris';

    const q = cashbookSearchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
    const matchSearch = !q ||
      (e.description && e.description.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
      (e.category && e.category.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
      (e.reference && e.reference.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
      (e.user && e.user.toLowerCase().includes(q));

    return matchType && matchMethod && matchSearch;
  });

  // Hitung total ringkasan hari ini (hanya yang statusnya aktif / bukan void)
  const activeDayEntries = currentDayEntries.filter(e => e.status !== 'void');

  const inEntries = activeDayEntries.filter(e => e.type === 'in');
  const outEntries = activeDayEntries.filter(e => e.type === 'out');

  const totalIn = inEntries.reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const totalOut = outEntries.reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const closingBalance = openingBalance + totalIn - totalOut;

  // Breakdown kanal pembayaran hari ini
  const cashIn = inEntries.filter(e => e.method === 'cash').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const cashOut = outEntries.filter(e => e.method === 'cash').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const cashNet = cashIn - cashOut;

  const bankIn = inEntries.filter(e => e.method === 'bank' || e.method === 'transfer').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const bankOut = outEntries.filter(e => e.method === 'bank' || e.method === 'transfer').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const bankNet = bankIn - bankOut;

  const qrisIn = inEntries.filter(e => e.method === 'qris').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const qrisOut = outEntries.filter(e => e.method === 'qris').reduce((sum, e) => sum + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const qrisNet = qrisIn - qrisOut;

  const isToday = targetDateKey === getLocalDateKey(new Date());

  return `
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6 space-y-6 pb-24 md:pb-8">
      
      <!-- Top Actions Bar -->
      <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 class="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="coins" class="w-6 h-6 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"></i>
            <span>Buku Kas Harian (Arus Kas Toko)</span>
          </h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
            Pencatatan saldo awal, uang masuk riil kasir/modal, pengeluaran operasional toko, dan saldo kas laci secara kronologis.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <!-- Button: Add Income -->
          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="openAddCashbookIncomeModal()"
            class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-500 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/20 active:scale-95 transition"
          >
            <i data-lucide="arrow-down-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Catat Uang Masuk</span>
          </button>

          <!-- Button: Add Expense -->
          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="openAddCashbookExpenseModal()"
            class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/20 active:scale-95 transition"
          >
            <i data-lucide="arrow-up-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Catat Uang Keluar</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Date Navigation Banner -->
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-4 shadow-sm flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div class="flex items-center space-x-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <i data-lucide="calendar" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">Tanggal Kas Terpilih:</div>
            <div class="font-extrabold text-slate-800 dark:text-white text-sm sm:text-base">${escapeHtml(dateDisplay)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center space-x-2 w-full sm:w-auto justify-end">
          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="shiftCashbookDate(-1)"
            class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 transition"
            title="Hari Sebelumnya"
          >
            <i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
          </button>

          <input 
            type="date"
            value="${escapeHtml(targetDateKey)}"
            onchange="setCashbookDate(this.value)"
            class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
          />

          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="shiftCashbookDate(1)"
            class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 transition"
            title="Hari Berikutnya"
          >
            <i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
          </button>

          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="setCashbookDate('${getLocalDateKey(new Date())}')"
            class="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition ${
              isToday 
                ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-300/40' 
                : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'
            }"
          >
            Hari Ini
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Main Financial Summary Cards (Saldo Awal, Masuk, Keluar, Saldo Akhir) -->
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        
        <!-- Saldo Awal -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-5 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Saldo Awal Kas</span>
            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-500 flex items-center justify-center">
              <i data-lucide="landmark" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white font-mono">${formatRupiah(openingBalance)}</div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Akumulasi saldo kas sebelum hari ini</p>
        </div>

        <!-- Total Uang Masuk -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-emerald-200/60 dark:border-emerald-900/40 p-5 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50/20 via-transparent to-transparent">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider">(+) Total Uang Masuk</span>
            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <i data-lucide="arrow-down-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-mono">${formatRupiah(totalIn)}</div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Penjualan + Modal Tambahan / Lainnya</p>
        </div>

        <!-- Total Uang Keluar -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-rose-200/60 dark:border-rose-900/40 p-5 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-rose-50/20 via-transparent to-transparent">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 uppercase tracking-wider">(-) Total Uang Keluar</span>
            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <i data-lucide="arrow-up-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 font-mono">-${formatRupiah(totalOut)}</div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Belanja Stok + Beban Operasional</p>
        </div>

        <!-- Saldo Kas Akhir -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border-2 border-indigo-500/40 dark:border-indigo-500/30 p-5 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-50/30 via-transparent to-transparent">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 uppercase tracking-wider">(=) Saldo Kas Akhir</span>
            <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-600 flex items-center justify-center">
              <i data-lucide="wallet-cards" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-mono">${formatRupiah(closingBalance)}</div>
          <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Saldo Awal + Masuk - Keluar</p>
        </div>

      </div>

      <!-- Payment Channels Breakdown (Kas Tunai Fisik Laci, Bank Transfer, QRIS) -->
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-5 shadow-sm">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 mb-4">
          <h3 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="layers" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600"></i>
            <span>Rincian Arus Kas Berdasarkan Jalur Pembayaran (Hari Ini)</span>
          </h3>
          <span class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Pemisahan fisik kas laci & saldo digital</span>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <!-- Kas Tunai (Laci Kasir) -->
          <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/60 space-y-2">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="banknote" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500"></i>
                <span>Kas Tunai (Laci Fisik)</span>
              </span>
              <span class="text-xs font-mono font-extrabold ${cashNet >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-600'}">
                ${formatRupiah(cashNet)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex justify-between pt-1 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/40">
              <span>Masuk: <b class="text-emerald-600">${formatRupiah(cashIn)}</b></span>
              <span>Keluar: <b class="text-rose-600">-${formatRupiah(cashOut)}</b></span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Rekening Bank / Transfer -->
          <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/60 space-y-2">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="credit-card" class="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500"></i>
                <span>Rekening Bank / Transfer</span>
              </span>
              <span class="text-xs font-mono font-extrabold ${bankNet >= 0 ? 'text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400' : 'text-rose-600'}">
                ${formatRupiah(bankNet)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex justify-between pt-1 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/40">
              <span>Masuk: <b class="text-blue-600">${formatRupiah(bankIn)}</b></span>
              <span>Keluar: <b class="text-rose-600">-${formatRupiah(bankOut)}</b></span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- QRIS / E-Wallet -->
          <div class="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/60 space-y-2">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                <i data-lucide="qr-code" class="w-4 h-4 text-purple-500"></i>
                <span>QRIS & Digital Wallet</span>
              </span>
              <span class="text-xs font-mono font-extrabold ${qrisNet >= 0 ? 'text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400' : 'text-rose-600'}">
                ${formatRupiah(qrisNet)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex justify-between pt-1 border-t border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700/40">
              <span>Masuk: <b class="text-purple-600">${formatRupiah(qrisIn)}</b></span>
              <span>Keluar: <b class="text-rose-600">-${formatRupiah(qrisOut)}</b></span>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Filters & Table of Daily Cash Mutations -->
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-4 shadow-sm flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-center justify-between">
        
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <select 
            onchange="cashbookTypeFilter = this.value; window.renderApp();"
            class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 font-bold focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all" ${cashbookTypeFilter === 'all' ? 'selected' : ''}>Semua Tipe Kas</option>
            <option value="in" ${cashbookTypeFilter === 'in' ? 'selected' : ''}>ðŸŸ¢ Uang Masuk (+)</option>
            <option value="out" ${cashbookTypeFilter === 'out' ? 'selected' : ''}>ðŸ”´ Uang Keluar (-)</option>
          </select>

          <select 
            onchange="cashbookMethodFilter = this.value; window.renderApp();"
            class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 font-bold focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all" ${cashbookMethodFilter === 'all' ? 'selected' : ''}>Semua Metode</option>
            <option value="cash" ${cashbookMethodFilter === 'cash' ? 'selected' : ''}>ðŸ’µ Tunai (Cash)</option>
            <option value="bank" ${cashbookMethodFilter === 'bank' ? 'selected' : ''}>ðŸ¦ Bank Transfer</option>
            <option value="qris" ${cashbookMethodFilter === 'qris' ? 'selected' : ''}>ðŸ“± QRIS / E-Wallet</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div class="relative w-full sm:w-72">
          <i data-lucide="search" class="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400"></i>
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Cari transaksi / keterangan kas..."
            value="${escapeHtml(cashbookSearchQuery)}"
            oninput="cashbookSearchQuery = this.value; window.debouncedRenderApp();"
            class="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
          />
        </div>

      </div>

      <!-- Cash Mutations Table -->
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="w-full text-left text-xs">
            <thead class="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 text-slate-500 uppercase font-semibold border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">
              <tr>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4">Waktu</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4">Tipe Kas</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4">Kategori</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4">Keterangan / Referensi</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4">Metode</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">Nominal (Rp)</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4">Operator</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">Aksi</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody class="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">
              ${filteredEntries.length > 0 ? filteredEntries.map(e => {
                const isVoid = e.status === 'void';
                const isIn = e.type === 'in';
                const isFromTrx = Boolean(e.reference && e.reference.startsWith('TRX'));
                const timeStr = e.date ? formatTimeOnly(e.date) : '-';

                const methodBadge = {
                  cash: '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300">Tunai</span>',
                  bank: '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-950/60 dark:text-blue-300">Bank</span>',
                  transfer: '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-950/60 dark:text-blue-300">Bank</span>',
                  qris: '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-950/60 dark:text-purple-300">QRIS</span>'
                }[e.method] || `<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">${escapeHtml(e.method || 'cash')}</span>`;

                return `
                  <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition ${isVoid ? 'opacity-60 bg-rose-50/20 dark:bg-rose-950/10' : ''}">
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-500">${escapeHtml(timeStr)}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                      ${isIn ? `
                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-950 dark:text-emerald-300">
                          <i data-lucide="arrow-down-left" class="w-3 h-3 mr-1"></i> Masuk
                        </span>
                      ` : `
                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-950 dark:text-rose-300">
                          <i data-lucide="arrow-up-right" class="w-3 h-3 mr-1"></i> Keluar
                        </span>
                      `}
                      ${isVoid ? `<span class="ml-1 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-rose-200 text-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-900 text-[9px] font-extrabold">VOID</span>` : ''}
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      ${escapeHtml(e.category || '-')}
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                      <div>${escapeHtml(e.description || '-')}</div>
                      ${e.reference ? `<div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">Ref: ${escapeHtml(e.reference)}</div>` : ''}
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">${methodBadge}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold ${isVoid ? 'line-through text-slate-400' : (isIn ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400')}">
                      ${isIn ? '+' : '-'}${formatRupiah(e.amount)}
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-slate-500">${escapeHtml(e.user || 'Admin')}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                      ${!isVoid && store.currentRole === 'admin' ? `
                        ${isFromTrx ? `
                          <button 
                            type="button" 
                            onclick="navigate('admin-transactions')" 
                            class="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-600 hover:text-indigo-800 hover:underline"
                            title="Buka menu Transaksi untuk void struk dan mengembalikan stok otomatis"
                          >
                            Lihat Struk
                          </button>
                        ` : `
                          <button 
                            type="button" 
                            onclick="promptVoidCashbookEntry('${escapeHtml(e.id)}')" 
                            class="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-rose-500 hover:text-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40 transition"
                            title="Batalkan (Void) Mutasi Kas Ini"
                          >
                            <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                          </button>
                        `}
                      ` : '-'}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                `;
              }).join('') : `
                <tr>
                  <td colspan="8" class="py-12 text-center text-slate-400">
                    <div class="max-w-xs mx-auto space-y-2">
                      <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                        <i data-lucide="inbox" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
                      </div>
                      <p class="font-bold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">Belum Ada Catatan Kas Pada Tanggal Ini</p>
                      <p class="text-xs">Uang masuk dari penjualan kasir otomatis masuk ke sini, atau klik tombol di atas untuk mencatat modal/biaya operasional.</p>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              `}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  `;
}

function formatTimeOnly(isoString) {
  try {
    const d = new Date(isoString);
    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '-';
    return d.toLocaleTimeString('id-ID', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  } catch (e) {
    return '-';
  }
}

async function handleSaveCashbookIncome(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  const store = window.store;

  const amount = Number(document.getElementById('csh-in-amount').value) || 0;
  const category = document.getElementById('csh-in-category').value;
  const method = document.getElementById('csh-in-method').value;
  const description = document.getElementById('csh-in-desc').value.trim();

  if (amount <= 0 || !description) {
    alert('Harap isi nominal dan keterangan dengan benar.');
    return;
  }

  const payload = {
    date: new Date().toISOString(),
    type: 'in',
    category,
    method,
    description,
    amount,
    user: store.cashierName || 'Admin'
  };

  const res = await store.addCashbookEntry(payload);
  if (res.success) {
    closeModal();
    window.renderApp();
    store.showRealtimeToast(`âœ… Uang masuk ${formatRupiah(amount)} berhasil dicatat di buku kas.`);
  } else {
    alert(res.error || 'Gagal menyimpan catatan kas.');
  }
}

async function handleSaveCashbookExpense(event) {
  event.preventDefault();
  const store = window.store;

  const amount = Number(document.getElementById('csh-out-amount').value) || 0;
  const category = document.getElementById('csh-out-category').value;
  const method = document.getElementById('csh-out-method').value;
  const description = document.getElementById('csh-out-desc').value.trim();

  if (amount <= 0 || !description) {
    alert('Harap isi nominal dan keterangan dengan benar.');
    return;
  }

  const payload = {
    date: new Date().toISOString(),
    type: 'out',
    category,
    method,
    description,
    amount,
    user: store.cashierName || 'Admin'
  };

  const res = await store.addCashbookEntry(payload);
  if (res.success) {
    closeModal();
    window.renderApp();
    store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ”´ Pengeluaran ${formatRupiah(amount)} berhasil dicatat di buku kas.`);
  } else {
    alert(res.error || 'Gagal menyimpan pengeluaran kas.');
  }
}

async function promptVoidCashbookEntry(entryId) {
  const store = window.store;
  if (store.currentRole !== 'admin') {
    alert('Hanya Admin yang dapat membatalkan mutasi kas.');
    return;
  }

  const reason = prompt('Masukkan alasan pembatalan (Void) catatan kas ini:');
  if (!reason || !reason.trim()) return;

  const res = await store.voidCashbookEntry(entryId, reason.trim());
  if (res.success) {
    window.renderApp();
    store.showRealtimeToast('Catatan kas berhasil dibatalkan (VOID).');
  } else {
    alert(res.error || 'Gagal membatalkan catatan kas.');
  }
}

/**
 * WARUNGKU POS - ADMIN DASHBOARD & ANALYTICS COMPONENT
 */

function renderAdminDashboard() {
  const store = window.store;

  // Calculate statistics using local timezone date keys
  const now = new Date();
  const todayDateStr = getLocalDateKey(now);

  // Active (non-void) transactions for financial & sales KPIs
  const validTransactions = store.transactions.filter(t => t.status !== 'void');

  // Transactions metrics
  const totalRevenue = validTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.total || 0), 0);
  const totalTax = validTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.tax || 0), 0);
  const totalNetSales = validTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (Number(t.subtotal || t.total || 0) - Number(t.discount || 0)), 0);
  const totalGrossProfit = validTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => {
    const netSale = Number(t.subtotal || t.total || 0) - Number(t.discount || 0);
    const cost = Number(t.totalCost || 0);
    return acc + (t.profit !== undefined ? Number(t.profit) : (netSale - cost));
  }, 0);
  const totalExpenses = store.expenses.reduce((acc, e) => acc + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const netProfit = totalGrossProfit - totalExpenses;
  const totalTransactions = validTransactions.length;
  const totalItemsSold = validTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (t.items || []).reduce((sum, i) => sum + Number(i.qty || 0), 0), 0);

  // Today's metrics (local timezone)
  const todayTransactions = validTransactions.filter(t => getLocalDateKey(t.date) === todayDateStr);
  const todayRevenue = todayTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.total || 0), 0);
  const todayExpenses = store.expenses.filter(e => getLocalDateKey(e.date) === todayDateStr).reduce((acc, e) => acc + Number(e.amount || 0), 0);
  const todayGrossProfit = todayTransactions.reduce((acc, t) => {
    const netSale = Number(t.subtotal || t.total || 0) - Number(t.discount || 0);
    const cost = Number(t.totalCost || 0);
    return acc + (t.profit !== undefined ? Number(t.profit) : (netSale - cost));
  }, 0);
  const todayNetProfit = todayGrossProfit - todayExpenses;

  // Low stock products
  const lowStockProducts = store.products.filter(p => p.stock <= p.minStock);

  // Top selling products calculation
  const productSalesMap = {};
  validTransactions.forEach(t => {
    (t.items || []).forEach(i => {
      if (!productSalesMap[i.id]) {
        productSalesMap[i.id] = { name: i.name, qty: 0, revenue: 0 };
      }
      productSalesMap[i.id].qty += i.qty;
      productSalesMap[i.id].revenue += i.subtotal;
    });
  });
  const topSelling = Object.values(productSalesMap).sort((a, b) => b.qty - a.qty).slice(0, 5);

  return `
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6 space-y-6 pb-24 md:pb-8">
      
      <!-- Welcome & Quick Header -->
      <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 to-indigo-950 p-6 rounded-3xl text-white shadow-xl">
        <div>
          <span class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-indigo-500/30 text-indigo-300 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">
            Admin Backoffice
          </span>
          <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold mt-2">Ringkasan Bisnis & Keuangan</h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-300 mt-1">Pantau omset, arus kas keluar-masuk, stok gudang, dan laba bersih toko secara realtime.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
          <button onclick="navigate('pos')" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-400 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/20 transition">
            <i data-lucide="shopping-cart" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Buka Kasir POS</span>
          </button>
          <button onclick="openAddExpenseModal()" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white font-semibold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 transition">
            <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Catat Pengeluaran</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- KPI Summary Cards (Grid) -->
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        
        <!-- Card 1: Total Pemasukan / Omset -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-5 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500">
            <span class="text-xs font-semibold">Total Pemasukan (Omset)</span>
            <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <i data-lucide="trending-up" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="mt-3">
            <h3 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${formatRupiah(totalRevenue)}</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center space-x-1">
              <i data-lucide="arrow-up-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Hari ini: ${formatRupiah(todayRevenue)}</span>
            </p>
            ${totalTax > 0 ? `<p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Net Exc. PPN: ${formatRupiah(totalNetSales)} (PPN: ${formatRupiah(totalTax)})</p>` : ''}
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Card 2: Total Pengeluaran Uang -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-5 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500">
            <span class="text-xs font-semibold">Total Pengeluaran Toko</span>
            <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-2xl bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <i data-lucide="arrow-down-right" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="mt-3">
            <h3 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">${formatRupiah(totalExpenses)}</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center space-x-1">
              <i data-lucide="arrow-down-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Hari ini: ${formatRupiah(todayExpenses)}</span>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Card 3: Keuntungan Bersih (Net Profit) -->
        <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-5 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-500">

            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="mt-3">
            <h3 class="text-2xl font-extrabold ${netProfit >= 0 ? 'text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400' : 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400'}">

            </h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
              Hari ini: <span class="font-bold ${todayNetProfit >= 0 ? 'text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400' : 'text-rose-600'}">${formatRupiah(todayNetProfit)}</span>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Card 4: Total Transaksi & Item Terjual -->

                    <p class="text-[11px] text-rose-500 font-semibold">Sisa: ${p.stock} ${escapeHtml(p.unit)} (Min: ${p.minStock})</p>
                  </div>
                  <button 
                    onclick="openRestockModal('${escapeHtml(p.id)}')"
                    class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center space-x-1"
                  >
                    <i data-lucide="plus" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
                    <span>Restock</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              `).join('') : `
                <div class="py-6 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">
                  <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-8 h-8 mx-auto text-emerald-500 mb-1"></i>
                  <span>Semua stok barang dalam kondisi aman.</span>
                </div>
              `}
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Top Selling Items Card -->
          <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-5 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm space-y-3">
            <h3 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-2">
              <i data-lucide="award" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-500"></i>
              <span>Produk Terlaris</span>
            </h3>

            <div class="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
              ${topSelling.length > 0 ? topSelling.map((item, idx) => `
                <div class="py-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div class="flex items-center space-x-2 min-w-0">
                    <span class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-bold text-[10px] flex items-center justify-center">
                      ${idx + 1}
                    </span>
                    <span class="text-xs font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 truncate">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span class="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 whitespace-nowrap">
                    ${item.qty} terjual
                  </span>
                </div>
              `).join('') : `
                <div class="py-4 text-center text-slate-400 text-xs">Belum ada data penjualan</div>
              `}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  `;
}

// Chart.js Renderer
function initSalesChart() {
  const canvas = document.getElementById('salesChart');
  if (!canvas) return;

  const store = window.store;
  const isDark = store.isDark;

  // Build last 7 days labels & values
  const labels = [];
  const revenueData = [];
  const profitData = [];

  for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
    const d = new Date();
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);
    const dateStr = getLocalDateKey(d);
    const label = d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric' });
    labels.push(label);

    const dayTrxs = store.transactions.filter(t => t.status !== 'void' && getLocalDateKey(t.date) === dateStr);
    const dayRev = dayTrxs.reduce((sum, t) => sum + Number(t.total || 0), 0);
    const dayProf = dayTrxs.reduce((sum, t) => sum + Number(t.profit || 0), 0);

    revenueData.push(dayRev);
    profitData.push(dayProf);
  }

  // Destroy previous chart if exists
  if (window._salesChartInstance) {
    window._salesChartInstance.destroy();
  }

  if (typeof Chart !== 'undefined') {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    window._salesChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
      type: 'bar',
      data: {
        labels: labels,
        datasets: [
          {
            label: 'Omset Penjualan (Rp)',
            data: revenueData,
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.8)',
            borderRadius: 8,
            barThickness: 16
          },
          {
            label: 'Keuntungan / Laba (Rp)',
            data: profitData,
            backgroundColor: 'rgba(99, 102, 241, 0.8)',
            borderRadius: 8,
            barThickness: 16
          }
        ]
      },
      options: {
        responsive: true,
        maintainAspectRatio: false,
        plugins: {
          legend: {
            position: 'top',
            labels: {
              color: isDark ? '#94a3b8' : '#475569',
              font: { family: "'Plus Jakarta Sans', sans-serif", size: 11, weight: '600' }
            }
          },
          tooltip: {
            callbacks: {
              label: function (context) {
                return context.dataset.label + ': ' + formatRupiah(context.raw);
              }
            }
          }
        },
        scales: {
          x: {
            grid: { display: false },
            ticks: { color: isDark ? '#64748b' : '#94a3b8', font: { size: 10 } }
          },
          y: {
            grid: { color: isDark ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.05)' },
            ticks: {
              color: isDark ? '#64748b' : '#94a3b8',
              font: { size: 10 },
              callback: function (value) {
                if (value >= 1000000) return 'Rp ' + (value / 1000000).toFixed(1) + 'jt';
                if (value >= 1000) return 'Rp ' + (value / 1000) + 'k';
                return 'Rp ' + value;
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }
}

/**
