            </button>
          </div>
        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();

  // Focus appropriate field immediately
  setTimeout(() => {
    if (prefillBarcode || isEdit) {
      const nameEl = document.getElementById('product-name-input');
      if (nameEl) nameEl.focus();
    } else {
      const barcodeEl = document.getElementById('product-barcode-input');
      if (barcodeEl) barcodeEl.focus();
    }
  }, 40);
}

function generateAutoSkuInModal() {
  const barcodeEl = document.getElementById('product-barcode-input');
  if (!barcodeEl) return;
  const randomCode = 'SKU-' + String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000));
  barcodeEl.value = randomCode;
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('barcode-scan-status');
  if (statusEl) {
    statusEl.className = 'text-[11px] font-semibold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 flex items-center space-x-1';
    statusEl.innerHTML = `<span>âœ“ Kode SKU otomatis dibuat: <b>${randomCode}</b></span>`;
  }
  const nameEl = document.getElementById('product-name-input');
  if (nameEl) nameEl.focus();
}

function handleProductModalBarcodeKey(e, currentProductId = '') {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') {
    // Prevent USB scanner's trailing Enter key from submitting the form prematurely!
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    const scannedCode = e.target.value.trim();
    if (!scannedCode) return;

    if (window.store.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') {
      soundFx.playBeep();
    }

    // Check if this barcode already belongs to an existing product
    const existing = window.store.products.find(
      item => String(item.barcode || '').trim().toLowerCase() === scannedCode.toLowerCase() && item.id !== currentProductId
    );
    if (existing) {
      if (confirm(`Barcode "${scannedCode}" sudah terdaftar untuk produk "${existing.name}".\n\nIngin membuka data produk "${existing.name}" untuk diedit?`)) {
        openProductModal(existing.id);
        return;
      }
    }

    const statusEl = document.getElementById('barcode-scan-status');
    if (statusEl) {
      statusEl.className = 'text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 flex items-center space-x-1';
      statusEl.innerHTML = `<span>âœ“ Barcode <b>${escapeHtml(scannedCode)}</b> berhasil terbaca! Silakan isi Nama Produk & Harga.</span>`;
    }

    const nameEl = document.getElementById('product-name-input');
    if (nameEl) {
      nameEl.focus();
      nameEl.select();
    }
  }
}

function handleAdminSearchBarcodeKey(e) {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') {
    e.preventDefault();
    const code = e.target.value.trim();
    if (!code) return;
    handleAdminBarcodeScanAction(code);
  }
}

function handleAdminBarcodeScanAction(scannedBarcode) {
  const cleanCode = String(scannedBarcode || '').trim();
  if (!cleanCode) return;

  // If Product Modal is already open, inject scanned barcode directly into #product-barcode-input!
  const modalBarcodeInput = document.getElementById('product-barcode-input');
  if (modalBarcodeInput) {
    modalBarcodeInput.value = cleanCode;
    handleProductModalBarcodeKey({ key: 'Enter', target: modalBarcodeInput, preventDefault: () => {}, stopPropagation: () => {} });
    return;
  }

  // Otherwise check if product already exists in catalog
  const existing = window.store.products.find(
    p => String(p.barcode || '').trim().toLowerCase() === cleanCode.toLowerCase() ||
         String(p.id || '').trim().toLowerCase() === cleanCode.toLowerCase()
  );

  if (existing) {
    if (window.store.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playBeep();
    productSearchQuery = '';
    window.renderApp();
    openProductModal(existing.id);
    window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ“¦ Barcode ditemukan: "${existing.name}"`);
  } else {
    if (window.store.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playBeep();
    productSearchQuery = '';
    window.renderApp();
    openProductModal(null, cleanCode);
    window.store.showRealtimeToast(`âœ¨ Barcode baru "${cleanCode}" terbaca! Silakan lengkapi Nama & Harga.`);
  }
}

async function handleSaveProduct(e, id) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const form = e.target;
  const productData = {
    id: id || undefined,
    name: form.name.value.trim(),
    barcode: form.barcode.value.trim(),
    category: form.category.value,
    costPrice: form.costPrice.value,
    sellPrice: form.sellPrice.value,
    stock: form.stock.value,
    minStock: form.minStock.value,
    unit: form.unit.value.trim() || 'pcs',
    image: form.image.value.trim()
  };

  await window.store.saveProduct(productData);
  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast(`âœ… Produk "${productData.name}" berhasil disimpan!`);
}

async function confirmDeleteProduct(id) {
  const prod = (window.store.products || []).find(item => item.id === id);
  const name = prod ? prod.name : id;
  if (confirm(`Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus produk "${name}"?`)) {
    await window.store.deleteProduct(id);
    window.renderApp();
    window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ—‘ï¸ Produk "${name}" telah dihapus.`);
  }
}

// Modal: Restock / Stock In & Out
function openRestockModal(productId) {
  const store = window.store;
  const p = store.products.find(item => item.id === productId);

  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `

          <h3 class="font-bold text-base text-slate-800 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="boxes" class="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500"></i>
            <span>Mutasi Stok: ${escapeHtml(p.name)}</span>
          </h3>
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="p-1.5 text-slate-400">
            <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onsubmit="handleSubmitRestock(event, '${p.id}')" class="space-y-4 text-xs">
          <div class="p-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl flex justify-between items-center font-semibold">
            <span class="text-slate-500">Stok Saat Ini:</span>
            <span class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-mono">${p.stock} ${escapeHtml(p.unit)}</span>

                <input type="radio" name="type" value="in" checked onchange="const el=document.getElementById('restock-expense-box'); if(el) el.style.display='block';" class="text-emerald-600" />

              <label class="flex items-center space-x-2 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 cursor-pointer bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800">
                <input type="radio" name="type" value="out" onchange="const el=document.getElementById('restock-expense-box'); if(el) el.style.display='none';" class="text-rose-600" />
                <span class="font-bold text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">- Barang Keluar (Rusak/Hilang)</span>
              </label>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="block font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Jumlah (${escapeHtml(p.unit)}) *</label>
            <input 
              type="number" 
              name="qty" 
              required 
              min="1" 
              value="10" 
              class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-bold text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="block font-semibold text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mb-1">Alasan / Catatan Supplier</label>
            <input 
              type="text" 
              name="reason" 
              placeholder="Contoh: Pembelian dari Supplier PT Beras Makmur"
              class="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-slate-800 dark:text-white font-medium focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div id="restock-expense-box" class="p-3 bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950/40 border border-indigo-200 dark:border-indigo-800/60 rounded-2xl">

            <button type="submit" class="px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold shadow-md">Simpan Mutasi</button>
          </div>
        </form>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  lucide.createIcons();
}

async function handleSubmitRestock(e, productId) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const form = e.target;
  const qty = form.qty.value;
  const type = form.type.value;
  const reason = form.reason.value;
  const recordExpense = type === 'in' && form.recordExpense.checked;

  await window.store.adjustStock(productId, qty, type, reason, recordExpense);
  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ“¦ Mutasi stok (${type === 'in' ? '+' : '-'}${qty}) berhasil disimpan!`);
}

async function handleSaveSettings(e) {
  e.preventDefault();
  const form = e.target;
  if (form.cashierName && form.cashierName.value.trim()) {
    window.store.setCashierName(form.cashierName.value.trim());
  }
  const printerNameVal = form.printerName ? form.printerName.value.trim() : '';
  localStorage.setItem('WARUNGKU_PRINTER_NAME', printerNameVal);
  if (form.autoPrintReceipt) {
    localStorage.setItem('pos_auto_print', form.autoPrintReceipt.checked ? 'true' : 'false');
  }
  await window.store.updateSettings({
    storeName: form.storeName.value.trim(),
    storePhone: form.storePhone.value.trim(),
    storeAddress: form.storeAddress.value.trim(),
    paperSize: form.paperSize.value,
    printerName: printerNameVal,
    taxRate: Number(form.taxRate.value) || 0,
    qrisMerchantName: form.qrisMerchantName.value.trim(),
    qrisNmid: form.qrisNmid.value.trim(),
    bankName: form.bankName.value.trim() || 'BCA',
    bankAccountNumber: form.bankAccountNumber.value.trim(),
    bankAccountHolder: form.bankAccountHolder.value.trim(),
    receiptFooter: form.receiptFooter.value.trim(),
    soundEnabled: Boolean(form.soundEnabled.checked),
    autoCloseEnabled: Boolean(form.autoCloseEnabled.checked),
    storeCloseTime: (form.storeCloseTime ? form.storeCloseTime.value.trim() : '22:00') || '22:00',
    adminPassword: form.adminPassword.value.trim(),
    cashierPin: form.cashierPin.value.trim()
  });
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast("âœ… Pengaturan toko & printer berhasil disimpan!");
}

async function detectInstalledPrinters() {
  const statusEl = document.getElementById('printer-detect-status');
  const inputEl = document.getElementById('settings-printer-name');
  if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = 'â³ Memindai printer USB VSC TM-58D PRO yang terhubung...';
  try {
    const res = await window.store.apiFetch('/api/printers');
    const data = await res.json();
    if (res.ok && data) {
      const printers = data.printers || [];
      const rec = data.recommendedPrinter || '';
      if (rec && inputEl) {
        inputEl.value = rec;
        localStorage.setItem('WARUNGKU_PRINTER_NAME', rec);
      }
      if (printers.length > 0) {
        const badges = printers.map(p => {
          const escaped = escapeHtml(p).replace(/'/g, "\\'");
          return `<button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('settings-printer-name').value='${escaped}'; localStorage.setItem('WARUNGKU_PRINTER_NAME','${escaped}');" class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-900/50 text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-200 font-mono text-[10px] hover:bg-emerald-200 transition">${escapeHtml(p)}</button>`;
        }).join(' ');
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.innerHTML = `âœ… Printer USB terdeteksi (klik untuk memilih): ${badges}`;
        }
      } else {
        if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = 'âš ï¸ Printer VSC TM-58D PRO belum terdeteksi di USB. Pastikan kabel USB terpasang & tombol power printer menyala.';
      }
    } else {
      if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = 'âš ï¸ Gagal mengambil daftar printer dari server.';
    }
  } catch (err) {
    if (statusEl) statusEl.innerHTML = 'âš ï¸ Gagal menghubungi server printer.';
  }
}

async function testThermalPrinterNow() {
  const inputEl = document.getElementById('settings-printer-name');
  if (inputEl && inputEl.value.trim()) {
    localStorage.setItem('WARUNGKU_PRINTER_NAME', inputEl.value.trim());
  }
  const sampleTrx = {
    id: 'TEST-' + String(Date.now()).slice(-6),
    date: new Date().toISOString(),
    cashier: window.store.cashierName || 'Kasir 1',
    customerName: 'TEST PRINTER VSC TM-58D',
    paymentMethod: 'Tunai',
    items: [
      { name: 'TEST CETAK VSC TM-58D PRO 58MM', qty: 1, sellPrice: 15000, subtotal: 15000 }
    ],
    subtotal: 15000,
    discount: 0,
    tax: 0,
    total: 15000,
    amountPaid: 20000,
    change: 5000
  };
  if (typeof printThermalReceiptNow === 'function') {
    await printThermalReceiptNow(sampleTrx);
  }
}

function handleImportFile(e) {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;

  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = async function(evt) {
    const result = await window.store.importBackupJSON(evt.target.result);
    alert(result.message);
    if (result.success) {
      window.renderApp();
    }
    e.target.value = '';
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
}

function confirmResetAll() {
  if (confirm("PERINGATAN: Semua data penjualan dan perubahan produk akan dikembalikan ke data awal demo. Lanjutkan?")) {
    window.store.resetToDefault();
  }
}

/**
 * WARUNGKU POS - ADMIN TRANSACTIONS HISTORY & REPORT COMPONENT
 */

let trxSearchQuery = '';
let trxPaymentFilter = 'all';
let trxDateFilter = 'today'; // 'today' | 'custom' | '7days' | 'month' | 'all'
let trxCustomDate = getLocalDateKey(new Date());

function setTrxCalendarDate(dateVal) {
  if (!dateVal) return;
  trxCustomDate = dateVal;
  trxDateFilter = 'custom';
  window.renderApp();
}

function shiftTrxDate(deltaDays) {
  try {
    const base = trxCustomDate || getLocalDateKey(new Date());
    const parts = base.split('-');
    const curDate = new Date(Number(parts[0]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[2]));
    curDate.setDate(curDate.getDate() + deltaDays);
    trxCustomDate = getLocalDateKey(curDate);
    trxDateFilter = 'custom';
    window.renderApp();
  } catch (e) {
    trxCustomDate = getLocalDateKey(new Date());
    trxDateFilter = 'custom';
    window.renderApp();
  }
}

function renderAdminTransactions() {
  const store = window.store;
  const now = new Date();
  const todayKey = getLocalDateKey(now);

  const filtered = store.transactions.filter(t => {
    const matchPayment = trxPaymentFilter === 'all' || t.paymentMethod === trxPaymentFilter;
    const matchSearch = t.id.toLowerCase().includes(trxSearchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (t.customerName && t.customerName.toLowerCase().includes(trxSearchQuery.toLowerCase())) ||
      (t.cashier && t.cashier.toLowerCase().includes(trxSearchQuery.toLowerCase()));

    let matchDate = true;
    if (trxDateFilter === 'today') {
      matchDate = getLocalDateKey(t.date) === todayKey;
    } else if (trxDateFilter === 'custom') {
      matchDate = getLocalDateKey(t.date) === trxCustomDate;
    } else if (trxDateFilter === '7days') {
      const d = new Date(t.date);
      const diffDays = (now - d) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
      matchDate = diffDays <= 7;
    } else if (trxDateFilter === 'month') {
      const d = new Date(t.date);
      matchDate = d.getFullYear() === now.getFullYear() && d.getMonth() === now.getMonth();
    }

    return matchPayment && matchSearch && matchDate;
  });

  const activeFiltered = filtered.filter(t => t.status !== 'void');
  const voidFiltered = filtered.filter(t => t.status === 'void');
  const totalOmset = activeFiltered.reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.total || 0), 0);
  const totalLaba = activeFiltered.reduce((acc, t) => acc + Number(t.profit || 0), 0);
  const totalItemsSold = activeFiltered.reduce((acc, t) => {
    const items = t.items || [];
    return acc + items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + Number(i.qty || 0), 0);
  }, 0);

  // Label periode terpilih
  let periodLabel = 'Hari Ini';
  if (trxDateFilter === 'all') periodLabel = 'Semua Waktu';
  else if (trxDateFilter === '7days') periodLabel = '7 Hari Terakhir';
  else if (trxDateFilter === 'month') periodLabel = 'Bulan Ini';
  else if (trxDateFilter === 'custom') {
    try {
      const parts = trxCustomDate.split('-');
      const d = new Date(Number(parts[0]), Number(parts[1]) - 1, Number(parts[2]));
      periodLabel = d.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });
    } catch (e) {
      periodLabel = trxCustomDate;
    }
  }

  return `
    <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6 space-y-6 pb-24 md:pb-8">
      
      <!-- Top Actions Bar -->
      <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 class="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="receipt" class="w-6 h-6 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400"></i>
            <span>Riwayat Transaksi Penjualan</span>
          </h2>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Daftar semua struk transaksi kasir, detail item pembelian, cetak ulang nota, atau batalkan transaksi.</p>
        </div>

        <button 
          onclick="exportTransactionsCSV()"
          class="px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center space-x-1.5 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/20 transition self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          <i data-lucide="file-spreadsheet" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
          <span>Export Laporan Excel (CSV)</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <!-- Date Navigator & Quick Filters Bar -->
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3 items-center justify-between">
        
        <!-- Date Selector with Prev / Next & Calendar Picker -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 w-full md:w-auto">
          <span class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 flex items-center space-x-1.5 shrink-0">
            <i data-lucide="calendar" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i>
            <span>Pilih Tanggal:</span>
          </span>

          <div class="flex items-center space-x-1">
            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="shiftTrxDate(-1)"
              class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 transition"
              title="Hari Sebelumnya"
            >
              <i data-lucide="chevron-left" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </button>

            <input 
              type="date"
              value="${escapeHtml(trxDateFilter === 'custom' ? trxCustomDate : todayKey)}"
              onchange="setTrxCalendarDate(this.value)"
              class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
            />

            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="shiftTrxDate(1)"
              class="p-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-200 transition"
              title="Hari Berikutnya"
            >
              <i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center space-x-1">
            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="trxDateFilter = 'today'; trxCustomDate = '${todayKey}'; window.renderApp();"
              class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition ${
                trxDateFilter === 'today'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'
              }"
            >
              Hari Ini
            </button>

            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="trxDateFilter = '7days'; window.renderApp();"
              class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition ${
                trxDateFilter === '7days'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'
              }"
            >

              onclick="trxDateFilter = 'month'; window.renderApp();"
              class="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition ${
                trxDateFilter === 'month'
                  ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'
              }"
            >

            <button

              placeholder="Cari No. Nota / Pelanggan..."

            />

          <select

            class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 font-bold focus:outline-none"
          >

          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Total Struk</span>
          <span class="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-slate-900 dark:text-white font-mono">${activeFiltered.length} struk</span>
          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mt-0.5 truncate">${escapeHtml(periodLabel)}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="p-3.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-200/60 dark:border-emerald-900/40 shadow-sm">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Total Omset</span>
          <span class="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-mono">${formatRupiah(totalOmset)}</span>
          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">Penjualan bersih</span>
        </div>

        <div class="p-3.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 shadow-sm">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Item Terjual</span>
          <span class="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-mono">${totalItemsSold} pcs</span>
          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">Total kuantitas barang</span>
        </div>

        <div class="p-3.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-2xl border border-rose-200/60 dark:border-rose-900/40 shadow-sm">

          <span class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">Dibatalkan / retur</span>
        </div>

          <span class="text-[10px] text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 block font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">Laba Bersih</span>
          <span class="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400 font-mono">${formatRupiah(totalLaba)}</span>

        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
          <table class="w-full text-left text-xs">
            <thead class="bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/60 text-slate-500 uppercase font-semibold border-b border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800">

                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">Laba / Profit</th>
                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">Aksi</th>
              </tr>

                  <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50/80 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition ${isVoid ? 'opacity-60 bg-rose-50/20 dark:bg-rose-950/10' : ''}">
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      <div class="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                        <span class="${isVoid ? 'line-through text-slate-400' : ''}">${escapeHtml(t.id)}</span>
                        ${isVoid ? `<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950 dark:text-rose-300 text-[9px] font-extrabold uppercase">VOID</span>` : ''}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 font-mono text-slate-500">${formatDateTime(t.date)}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                      <div class="font-semibold">${escapeHtml(t.customerName || 'Umum')}</div>
                      <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">${escapeHtml(t.cashier)}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                      <div class="font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">${totalItems} item</div>
                      <div class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate max-w-[200px]" title="${escapeHtml(itemSummary)}">${escapeHtml(itemSummary)}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4">
                      <span class="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold uppercase ${t.paymentMethod === 'cash'
        ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300'
        : t.paymentMethod === 'qris'
          ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 dark:bg-rose-950/60 dark:text-rose-300'
          : 'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 dark:text-indigo-300'
      }">
                        ${escapeHtml(t.paymentMethod)}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold ${isVoid ? 'line-through text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-900 dark:text-white'}">${formatRupiah(t.total)}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono font-semibold ${isVoid ? 'line-through text-slate-400' : 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'}">${isVoid ? 'Rp 0 (Void)' : `${t.profit >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${formatRupiah(t.profit)}`}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center">
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5">
                        <button 
                          onclick="openReceiptModalById('${t.id}')"
                          title="Lihat / Cetak Ulang Struk"
                          class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 transition"
                        >
                          <i data-lucide="printer" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                        </button>
                        ${!isVoid ? `
                          <button 
                            onclick="confirmVoidTransaction('${t.id}')"
                            title="Void / Batalkan Transaksi & Kembalikan Stok (Audit Trail)"
                            class="p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 hover:bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/50 text-rose-600 transition"
                          >
                            <i data-lucide="ban" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                          </button>
                        ` : `
                          <span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 text-[10px] font-bold" title="Transaksi telah dibatalkan (Void)">
                            Dibatalkan
                          </span>
                        `}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                `;
  }).join('') : `
                <tr>
                  <td colspan="8" class="py-12 text-center text-slate-400">Tidak ada riwayat transaksi ditemukan.</td>
                </tr>
              `}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  `;
}

async function confirmVoidTransaction(trxId) {
  const reason = prompt(`Masukkan alasan pembatalan (VOID) untuk nota "${trxId}":\n(Stok barang akan dikembalikan & riwayat transaksi tetap tersimpan dengan status VOID untuk audit)`, 'Salah input item / pembatalan pelanggan');
  if (reason === null) return;
  await window.store.voidTransaction(trxId, reason.trim() || 'Dibatalkan oleh Admin');
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ”„ Transaksi ${trxId} ditandai VOID & stok dikembalikan.`);
}

function exportTransactionsCSV() {
  const store = window.store;
  if (store.transactions.length === 0) {
    alert("Belum ada transaksi untuk diexport!");
    return;
  }

  let csv = "\uFEFFNo. Nota,Tanggal,Status,Kasir,Pelanggan,Total Item,Subtotal,Diskon,PPN,Total Bayar,Modal HPP,Laba,Metode Bayar\n";
  store.transactions.forEach(t => {
    const totalItems = (t.items || []).reduce((s, i) => s + Number(i.qty || 0), 0);
    const dateFormatted = `"${formatDateTime(t.date)}"`;
    const statusLabel = (t.status || 'completed').toUpperCase();
    csv += `"${t.id}",${dateFormatted},"${statusLabel}","${(t.cashier || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}","${(t.customerName || 'Umum').replace(/"/g, '""')}",${totalItems},${t.subtotal || 0},${t.discount || 0},${t.tax || 0},${t.total || 0},${t.totalCost || 0},${t.profit || 0},"${t.paymentMethod}"\n`;
  });

  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `laporan_penjualan_${getLocalDateKey(new Date())}.csv`;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

let activePaymentTab = 'cash'; // 'cash' | 'qris' | 'transfer'
let cashAmountGiven = 0;
let lastCompletedTransaction = null;
let currentReceiptWidth = '58mm';

// ==================== CHECKOUT MODAL ====================
function openCheckoutModal() {
  const store = window.store;
  if (store.cart.length === 0) return;

  // Close mobile cart bottom sheet if open so it doesn't overlap
  if (typeof isMobileCartOpen !== 'undefined' && isMobileCartOpen) {
    isMobileCartOpen = false;
    window.renderApp();
  }

  cashAmountGiven = 0; // Kosong secara default tanpa template
  activePaymentTab = 'cash';
  currentReceiptWidth = store.settings.paperSize || '58mm';

  renderCheckoutModalDOM();
}

              <!-- QR Code Canvas Display -->
              <div class="p-3 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-md border border-slate-200 flex flex-col items-center">
                <div id="qris-qrcode-box" class="w-44 h-44 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <!-- QR generated dynamically -->
                </div>
                <p class="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-600 mt-2 font-mono">${formatRupiah(calc.total)}</p>
              </div>

              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 text-center">
                Minta pelanggan scan QRIS di atas melalui GoPay, OVO, Dana, ShopeePay, BCA, atau Mobile Banking lainnya.
              </p>
            </div>
          ` : ''}

          <!-- TAB 3: TRANSFER / E-WALLET -->
          ${activePaymentTab === 'transfer' ? `
            <div class="space-y-3">
              <div class="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">${escapeHtml(store.settings.bankName || 'BCA')}</div>
                  <div class="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">${escapeHtml(store.settings.bankAccountNumber || '8830-1928-4412')}</div>
                  <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">a.n ${escapeHtml(store.settings.bankAccountHolder || store.settings.storeName)}</div>
                </div>
                <button onclick="copyToClipboard('${escapeHtml((store.settings.bankAccountNumber || '883019284412').replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''))}', 'No. Rekening disalin!')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs hover:bg-slate-100">
                  Salin
                </button>
              </div>

              <div class="p-3.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white">DANA / GoPay / OVO</div>
                  <div class="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">${escapeHtml(store.settings.storePhone)}</div>
                  <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">a.n ${escapeHtml(store.settings.storeName)}</div>
                </div>
                <button onclick="copyToClipboard('${escapeHtml(store.settings.storePhone)}', 'No. HP E-Wallet disalin!')" class="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-white dark:bg-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs hover:bg-slate-100">
                  Salin
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ` : ''}

          <!-- Submit Button -->
          <button 
            id="checkout-submit-btn"
            onclick="submitCheckout()"
            ${activePaymentTab === 'cash' && (cashAmountGiven < calc.total || cashAmountGiven <= 0) ? 'disabled' : ''}
            class="w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold text-sm shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/30 flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition"
          >
            <i data-lucide="check-circle" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            <span>Selesaikan Transaksi (${formatRupiah(calc.total)})</span>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  lucide.createIcons();

  if (activePaymentTab === 'qris') {
    generateQrisQRCode(calc.total);
  }
}

function switchPaymentTab(tab) {
  activePaymentTab = tab;
  renderCheckoutModalDOM();
}

// Live Handlers for Custom Manual Cash Input
function setQuickCash(amount) {
  cashAmountGiven = Number(amount) || 0;
  const input = document.getElementById('cash-input');
  if (input) {
    input.value = cashAmountGiven;
  }
  updateCashChangeUI();
}

function handleCashInputLive(val) {
  cashAmountGiven = Number(val) || 0;
  updateCashChangeUI();
}

function handleClearCashInput() {
  cashAmountGiven = 0;
  const input = document.getElementById('cash-input');
  if (input) {
    input.value = '';
    input.focus();
  }
  updateCashChangeUI();
}

function pressKeypad(key) {
  const input = document.getElementById('cash-input');
  let currentVal = input ? String(input.value || '') : '';

  if (key === 'C') {
    currentVal = '';
  } else if (key === 'BACKSPACE') {
    currentVal = currentVal.slice(0, -1);
  } else {
    if (currentVal === '0') currentVal = '';
    currentVal += key;
  }

  cashAmountGiven = Number(currentVal) || 0;
  if (input) {
    input.value = currentVal;
  }
  updateCashChangeUI();
}

function updateCashChangeUI() {
  const calc = window.store.getCartCalculations();
  const change = cashAmountGiven - calc.total;
  const isEnough = cashAmountGiven >= calc.total && cashAmountGiven > 0;
  const container = document.getElementById('cash-change-container');
  const submitBtn = document.getElementById('checkout-submit-btn');

  if (container) {
    if (cashAmountGiven <= 0) {
      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
          Silakan masukkan jumlah uang yang diterima dari customer
        </div>
      `;
    } else {
      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="p-3.5 rounded-2xl ${
          isEnough
            ? 'bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800/60'
            : 'bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-200 dark:border-rose-800/60'
        }">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
            <span class="text-xs font-bold ${isEnough ? 'text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300' : 'text-rose-800 dark:text-rose-300'}">
              ${isEnough ? 'Kembalian:' : 'Uang Kurang:'}
            </span>
            <span class="text-lg font-extrabold ${isEnough ? 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400'} font-mono">
              ${formatRupiah(Math.abs(change))}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }
  }

  if (submitBtn) {
    submitBtn.disabled = !isEnough;
  }
}

function roundUp(num, factor) {
  return Math.ceil(num / factor) * factor;
}

function generateQrisQRCode(nominal) {
  setTimeout(() => {
    const box = document.getElementById('qris-qrcode-box');
    if (!box) return;
    box.innerHTML = '';
    
    // Standard dummy QR payload representation for POS demonstration
    const payload = `00020101021226${nominal}5204581253033605405${nominal}5802ID5914${window.store.settings.storeName}6007JAKARTA6304`;
    
    if (typeof QRCode !== 'undefined') {
      new QRCode(box, {
        text: payload,
        width: 160,
        height: 160,
        colorDark: "#0f172a",
        colorLight: "#ffffff",
        correctLevel: QRCode.CorrectLevel.M
      });
    } else {
      // Fallback SVG QR
      box.innerHTML = `<img src="https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=160x160&data=${encodeURIComponent(payload)}" alt="QRIS" class="w-40 h-40" />`;
    }
  }, 50);
}

async function submitCheckout() {
  const store = window.store;
  const submitBtn = document.getElementById('checkout-submit-btn');
  if (submitBtn) {
    submitBtn.disabled = true;
    submitBtn.innerText = 'Memproses...';
  }

  try {
    const trx = await store.processCheckout(activePaymentTab, cashAmountGiven, store.customerName);
    if (!trx) {
      if (submitBtn) {
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.innerText = 'Selesaikan Pembayaran';
      }
      return;
    }

    lastCompletedTransaction = trx;
    closeModal();
    openPaymentSuccessModal(trx);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Checkout error:", err);
    alert("Terjadi kesalahan saat memproses pembayaran: " + (err.message || err));
    if (submitBtn) {
      submitBtn.disabled = false;
      submitBtn.innerText = 'Selesaikan Pembayaran';
    }
  }
}

// ==================== THERMAL RECEIPT HTML & ESC/POS ENGINE (VSC TM-58D PRO) ====================
function buildReceiptInnerHtml(trx) {
  if (!trx) return '';
  const store = window.store;
  const s = store?.settings || {};
  const trxDate = trx.date || trx.createdAt || new Date().toISOString();
  const trxCashier = trx.cashier || trx.cashierName || store?.cashierName || 'Kasir';
  const items = Array.isArray(trx.items) ? trx.items : [];

  return `
    <div style="color:#000000;background:#ffffff;font-family:'Courier New',Consolas,monospace;font-size:11px;line-height:1.35;">
      <div style="text-align:center;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:1px dashed #000000;">
        <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:13px;text-transform:uppercase;">${escapeHtml(s.storeName || 'WarungKu POS')}</div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;margin-top:2px;">${escapeHtml(s.storeAddress || '')}</div>
        <div style="font-size:10.5px;">Telp/WA: ${escapeHtml(s.storePhone || '')}</div>
      </div>

      <div style="padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed #000000;font-size:10.5px;">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span>No. Nota:</span>
          <span style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(trx.id || '-')}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span>Tanggal:</span>
          <span>${formatDateTime(trxDate)}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span>Kasir:</span>
          <span>${escapeHtml(trxCashier)}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span>Pelanggan:</span>
          <span>${escapeHtml(trx.customerName || 'Umum')}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed #000000;font-size:10.5px;">
        ${items.map(item => {
          const unitPrice = item.sellPrice !== undefined ? item.sellPrice : (item.price || 0);
          const lineSub = item.subtotal !== undefined ? item.subtotal : (Number(item.qty || 1) * Number(unitPrice));
          return `
            <div style="margin-bottom:3px;">
              <div style="font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(item.name || 'Produk')}</div>
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding-left:4px;">
                <span>${item.qty || 1} x ${formatRupiah(unitPrice)}</span>
                <span style="font-weight:700;">${formatRupiah(lineSub)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('')}
      </div>

      <div style="padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px dashed #000000;font-size:10.5px;">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span>Subtotal:</span>
          <span>${formatRupiah(trx.subtotal || 0)}</span>
        </div>
        ${Number(trx.discount || 0) > 0 ? `
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
            <span>Diskon:</span>
            <span>-${formatRupiah(trx.discount)}</span>
          </div>
        ` : ''}
        ${Number(trx.tax || 0) > 0 ? `
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
            <span>PPN:</span>
            <span>${formatRupiah(trx.tax)}</span>
          </div>
        ` : ''}
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-weight:800;font-size:12px;padding-top:3px;margin-top:2px;border-top:1px solid #000000;">
          <span>TOTAL:</span>
          <span>${formatRupiah(trx.total || 0)}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding-top:2px;">
          <span>Metode:</span>
          <span style="text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:700;">${escapeHtml(trx.paymentMethod || 'TUNAI')}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;">
          <span>Bayar:</span>
          <span>${formatRupiah(trx.amountPaid !== undefined ? trx.amountPaid : (trx.total || 0))}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-weight:800;">
          <span>Kembali:</span>
          <span>${formatRupiah(trx.change || 0)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="padding-top:6px;text-align:center;font-size:10px;white-space:pre-line;">
        ${escapeHtml(s.receiptFooter || 'Terima kasih atas kunjungan Anda!')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
}

// ==================== PAYMENT SUCCESS & PRINT OPTION MODAL ====================
function openPaymentSuccessModal(trx) {
  lastCompletedTransaction = trx;
  const store = window.store;
  currentReceiptWidth = store?.settings?.paperSize || '58mm';
  const autoPrint = localStorage.getItem('pos_auto_print') === 'true';

  // Immediately populate #pos-print-area so 1-click print works right away
  preparePrintArea(trx);

  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-6 space-y-4 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        
        <!-- Success Icon -->
        <div class="text-center space-y-1.5">
          <div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-3xl bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 mx-auto flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-emerald-500/20">
            <i data-lucide="check-circle-2" class="w-8 h-8"></i>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 class="font-extrabold text-xl text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Pembayaran Berhasil!</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">${escapeHtml(trx.id)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Detail Pembayaran & Kembalian -->
        <div class="p-4 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/80 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 space-y-2 text-xs">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            <span>Total Belanja:</span>
            <span class="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-mono">${formatRupiah(trx.total)}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            <span>Uang Diterima (${escapeHtml(trx.paymentMethod.toUpperCase())}):</span>
            <span class="font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 text-sm font-mono">${formatRupiah(trx.amountPaid)}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="pt-2 border-t border-dashed border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 flex justify-between items-center">
            <span class="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-800 dark:text-emerald-300">Kembalian:</span>
            <span class="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-mono">${formatRupiah(trx.change)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- VSC TM-58D PRO Thermal Printer Actions -->
        <div class="space-y-2 pt-1">
          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="printThermalReceiptNow()"
            class="w-full py-3.5 px-4 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-extrabold text-xs sm:text-sm flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/30 transition active:scale-95"
          >
            <i data-lucide="printer" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
            <span>Cetak Struk Sekarang (VSC TM-58D 58mm)</span>
          </button>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="printReceipt()"
              class="py-2.5 px-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 transition"
              title="Cetak via Dialog Browser (Ctrl+P)"
            >
              <i data-lucide="file-text" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Dialog Print</span>
            </button>

            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="printReceiptBluetooth()"
              class="py-2.5 px-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 hover:bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950/60 text-indigo-700 dark:text-indigo-300 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 transition"
              title="Cetak via Bluetooth ke VSC TM-58D PRO"
            >
              <i data-lucide="bluetooth" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Cetak BT</span>
            </button>

            <button 
              type="button"
              onclick="openReceiptModal(lastCompletedTransaction)"
              class="py-2.5 px-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 transition"
            >
              <i data-lucide="eye" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Lihat Nota</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Toggle Auto-Print Saat Pembayaran Selesai -->
        <div class="pt-1 flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800/50 border border-slate-200/70 dark:border-slate-800 text-[11px]">
          <label class="flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold">
            <input 
              type="checkbox" 
              ${autoPrint ? 'checked' : ''} 
              onchange="toggleAutoPrintSetting(this.checked)"
              class="rounded text-emerald-600 focus:ring-emerald-500"
            />
            <span>Otomatis cetak struk setiap selesai bayar</span>
          </label>
          <span class="text-[10px] font-mono text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">58mm</span>
        </div>

        <!-- Bottom Close & WhatsApp -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-1">
          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="shareReceiptWhatsApp()"
            class="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 dark:border-emerald-800 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 hover:bg-emerald-50 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950/40 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 transition"
          >
            <i data-lucide="send" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
            <span>Kirim WhatsApp</span>
          </button>

          <button 
            type="button"
            onclick="closeModal();"
            class="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 transition"
          >
            <span>Transaksi Baru</span>
            <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();

  // Trigger auto-print if enabled
  if (autoPrint) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      printThermalReceiptNow();
    }, 250);
  }
}

function toggleAutoPrintSetting(enabled) {
  localStorage.setItem('pos_auto_print', enabled ? 'true' : 'false');
  window.store.showRealtimeToast(enabled ? 'ðŸ–¨ï¸ Auto-Print Struk diaktifkan!' : 'Auto-Print Struk dinonaktifkan.');
}

// ==================== RECEIPT MODAL ====================
function openReceiptModalById(trxId) {
  const store = window.store;
  const trx = store?.transactions?.find(t => t.id === trxId);
  if (trx) {
    openReceiptModal(trx);
  }
}

function openReceiptModal(trx) {
  if (!trx) return;
  lastCompletedTransaction = trx;
  const store = window.store;
  currentReceiptWidth = store?.settings?.paperSize || '58mm';
  preparePrintArea(trx);

  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto">
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        
        <!-- Header -->
        <div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="receipt" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600"></i>
            <div>
              <h3 class="font-bold text-base text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Struk Pembayaran (VSC TM-58D PRO)</h3>
              <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Ukuran Kertas Thermal: ${currentReceiptWidth}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-200">
            <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

        <!-- Receipt Content Wrapper -->
        <div class="p-6 space-y-4 max-h-[65vh] overflow-y-auto bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950/50">
          
          <!-- Thermal Paper Struk Card -->
          <div id="printable-receipt" class="bg-white text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm font-receipt text-xs mx-auto ${currentReceiptWidth === '80mm' ? 'print-width-80mm max-w-[300px]' : 'max-w-[240px]'}">
            ${buildReceiptInnerHtml(trx)}
          </div>

        </div>

        <!-- Action Buttons -->
        <div class="p-4 border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 space-y-2">
          <button 
            onclick="printThermalReceiptNow()"
            class="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-extrabold text-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/20 transition"
          >
            <i data-lucide="printer" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
            <span>Cetak Langsung ke VSC TM-58D (USB ESC/POS)</span>
          </button>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            <button 
              onclick="printReceipt()"
              class="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-800 text-white font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 transition"
            >
              <i data-lucide="file-text" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Print Browser</span>
            </button>

            <button 
              onclick="printReceiptBluetooth()"
              class="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 transition"
            >
              <i data-lucide="bluetooth" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>Print Bluetooth</span>
            </button>

            <button 
              onclick="shareReceiptWhatsApp()"
              class="py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl bg-teal-600 hover:bg-teal-500 text-white font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-center space-x-1 transition"
            >
              <i data-lucide="send" class="w-3.5 h-3.5"></i>
              <span>WhatsApp</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <button 
            onclick="closeModal()"
            class="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 transition"
          >
            <i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400"></i>
            <span>Selesai / Transaksi Berikutnya</span>
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
}

function preparePrintArea(trx = null) {
  const targetTrx = trx || lastCompletedTransaction;
  const store = window.store;
  const widthMode = store?.settings?.paperSize || '58mm';

  let printArea = document.getElementById('pos-print-area');
  if (!printArea) {
    printArea = document.createElement('div');
    printArea.id = 'pos-print-area';
    document.body.appendChild(printArea);
  }

  const receiptEl = document.getElementById('printable-receipt');
  if (receiptEl) {
    printArea.innerHTML = receiptEl.innerHTML;
    printArea.className = widthMode === '80mm' ? 'print-width-80mm' : '';
  } else if (targetTrx) {
    printArea.innerHTML = buildReceiptInnerHtml(targetTrx);
    printArea.className = widthMode === '80mm' ? 'print-width-80mm' : '';
  }
}

function printReceipt() {
  preparePrintArea(lastCompletedTransaction);
  window.print();
}

async function printThermalReceiptNow(customTrx = null) {
  const trx = customTrx || lastCompletedTransaction;
  if (!trx) return;
  preparePrintArea(trx);

  const store = window.store;
  try {
    const res = await store.apiFetch('/api/print', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        transaction: trx,
        printerName: store.settings.printerName || ''
      })
    });
    const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (res.ok && data.success) {
      store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ–¨ï¸ Struk berhasil dicetak ke ${data.printerName}!`);
      return;
    }
    // Fallback to browser thermal print dialog if direct raw spooler isn't configured or returns error
    window.print();
  } catch (e) {
    window.print();
  }
}

async function printReceiptBluetooth(customTrx = null) {
  const trx = customTrx || lastCompletedTransaction;
  if (!trx) return;

  if (!navigator.bluetooth) {
    alert('Browser ini tidak mendukung Web Bluetooth. Gunakan Google Chrome / Microsoft Edge atau tombol Cetak USB.');
    return;
  }

  try {
    const store = window.store;
    // Standard BLE Thermal Printer Services (used by VSC TM-58D PRO, RPP02N, MTP-II)
    const device = await navigator.bluetooth.requestDevice({
      acceptAllDevices: true,
      optionalServices: [
        '000018f0-0000-1000-8000-00805f9b34fb',
        'e7810a71-73ae-499d-8c15-faa9aef0c3f2',
        '49535343-fe7d-4ae5-8fa9-9fafd205e455'
      ]
    });

    const server = await device.gatt.connect();
    const services = await server.getPrimaryServices();
    let writeChar = null;

    for (const srv of services) {
      const chars = await srv.getCharacteristics();
      for (const ch of chars) {
        if (ch.properties.write || ch.properties.writeWithoutResponse) {
          writeChar = ch;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (writeChar) break;
    }

    if (!writeChar) {
      throw new Error('Karakteristik tulis Bluetooth pada printer tidak ditemukan.');
    }

    const escposBytes = buildClientEscPosBytes(trx, store.settings);
    // Send in 100-byte chunks for BLE buffer compatibility
    const chunkSize = 100;
    for (let i = 0; i < escposBytes.length; i += chunkSize) {
      const chunk = escposBytes.slice(i, i + chunkSize);
      if (writeChar.properties.writeWithoutResponse) {
        await writeChar.writeValueWithoutResponse(chunk);
      } else {
        await writeChar.writeValue(chunk);
      }
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 25));
    }

    store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ–¨ï¸ Struk berhasil dicetak via Bluetooth (${device.name || 'VSC TM-58D'})!`);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err && err.name !== 'NotFoundError') {
      alert('Gagal mencetak via Bluetooth: ' + (err.message || err));
    }
  }
}

function buildClientEscPosBytes(trx, settings = {}) {
  const width = 32;
  const sep = '-'.repeat(width);
  const padLR = (l, r) => {
    const ls = String(l || '');
    const rs = String(r || '');
    const maxL = Math.max(1, width - rs.length - 1);
    const cleanL = ls.length > maxL ? ls.slice(0, maxL) : ls;
    return cleanL + ' '.repeat(Math.max(1, width - cleanL.length - rs.length)) + rs;
  };

  let text = '';
  text += '\x1B\x40'; // Init
  text += '\x1B\x61\x01'; // Center
  text += '\x1B\x45\x01' + String(settings.storeName || 'WARUNGKU POS').toUpperCase() + '\x1B\x45\x00\n';
  if (settings.storeAddress) text += settings.storeAddress + '\n';
  if (settings.storePhone) text += 'Telp: ' + settings.storePhone + '\n';
  text += sep + '\n';
  text += '\x1B\x61\x00'; // Left
  text += padLR('Nota:', trx.id) + '\n';
  text += padLR('Tgl :', formatDateTime(trx.date)) + '\n';
  text += padLR('Kasir:', trx.cashier) + '\n';
  text += sep + '\n';

  (trx.items || []).forEach(item => {
    text += String(item.name || '').slice(0, width) + '\n';
    text += padLR(`  ${item.qty} x ${formatRupiah(item.sellPrice)}`, formatRupiah(item.subtotal)) + '\n';
  });

  text += sep + '\n';
  text += padLR('Subtotal', formatRupiah(trx.subtotal)) + '\n';
  if (trx.discount > 0) text += padLR('Diskon', '-' + formatRupiah(trx.discount)) + '\n';
  if (trx.tax > 0) text += padLR('PPN', formatRupiah(trx.tax)) + '\n';
  text += '\x1B\x45\x01' + padLR('TOTAL', formatRupiah(trx.total)) + '\x1B\x45\x00\n';
  text += padLR(`Bayar (${String(trx.paymentMethod || 'CASH').toUpperCase()})`, formatRupiah(trx.amountPaid)) + '\n';
  text += padLR('Kembali', formatRupiah(trx.change)) + '\n';
  text += sep + '\n';
  text += '\x1B\x61\x01';
  text += String(settings.receiptFooter || 'Terima kasih!') + '\n\n\n\n';

  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  return encoder.encode(text);
}

window.addEventListener('beforeprint', () => preparePrintArea(lastCompletedTransaction));

function shareReceiptWhatsApp() {
  if (!lastCompletedTransaction) return;
  const trx = lastCompletedTransaction;
  const store = window.store;

  let msg = `*${store.settings.storeName}*\n`;
  msg += `${store.settings.storeAddress}\n`;
  msg += `Telp: ${store.settings.storePhone}\n`;
  msg += `--------------------------------\n`;
  msg += `No. Nota: ${trx.id}\n`;
  msg += `Tanggal : ${formatDateTime(trx.date)}\n`;
  msg += `Kasir   : ${trx.cashier}\n`;
  msg += `Pelanggan: ${trx.customerName}\n`;
  msg += `--------------------------------\n`;
  trx.items.forEach(item => {
    msg += `${item.name}\n  ${item.qty} x ${formatRupiah(item.sellPrice)} = ${formatRupiah(item.subtotal)}\n`;
  });
  msg += `--------------------------------\n`;
  msg += `Subtotal : ${formatRupiah(trx.subtotal)}\n`;
  if (trx.discount > 0) msg += `Diskon   : -${formatRupiah(trx.discount)}\n`;
  if (trx.tax > 0) msg += `PPN      : ${formatRupiah(trx.tax)}\n`;
  msg += `*TOTAL    : ${formatRupiah(trx.total)}*\n`;
  msg += `Bayar (${trx.paymentMethod.toUpperCase()}): ${formatRupiah(trx.amountPaid)}\n`;
  msg += `Kembali  : ${formatRupiah(trx.change)}\n`;
  msg += `--------------------------------\n`;
  msg += `${store.settings.receiptFooter}\n`;

  const encoded = encodeURIComponent(msg);
  window.open(`https://api.whatsapp.com/send?text=${encoded}`, '_blank');
}

// ==================== ROLE SWITCH MODAL ====================
function openRoleSwitchModal() {
  const store = window.store;
  const targetRole = store.currentRole === 'cashier' ? 'admin' : 'cashier';
  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-6 space-y-4 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        
        <div class="text-center">
          <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl ${targetRole === 'admin' ? 'bg-indigo-100 dark:bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400' : 'bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'} mx-auto flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
            <i data-lucide="${targetRole === 'admin' ? 'shield-check' : 'shopping-cart'}" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
          </div>
          <h3 class="font-bold text-lg text-slate-800 dark:text-white">
            Ganti Mode ke ${targetRole === 'admin' ? 'Admin Toko' : 'Kasir POS'}
          </h3>
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
            ${targetRole === 'admin' ? 'Masukkan Password Admin untuk mengakses laporan keuangan & stok.' : 'Masukkan nama operator & PIN Kasir untuk memulai sesi kasir.'}
          </p>
        </div>

        ${targetRole === 'cashier' ? `
          <div>
            <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1.5">
              Nama Operator Kasir
            </label>
            <input 
              type="text" 
              id="role-cashier-name-input"
              value="${escapeHtml(store.cashierName || 'Kasir 1')}"
              placeholder="Contoh: Kasir 1 / Budi"
              class="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-center text-sm font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>
        ` : ''}

        <div>
          <label class="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 mb-1.5">
            ${targetRole === 'admin' ? 'Password Admin' : 'PIN Kasir'}
          </label>
          <input 
            type="password" 
            id="role-auth-input"
            placeholder="${targetRole === 'admin' ? 'Ketik password...' : 'Masukkan PIN kasir...'}"
            class="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-2xl text-center text-lg font-bold text-slate-800 dark:text-white focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:outline-none tracking-widest"
            onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter') submitRoleSwitch('${targetRole}')"
            autofocus
          />
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2">
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">
            Batal
          </button>
          <button onclick="submitRoleSwitch('${targetRole}')" class="py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 dark:bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:bg-slate-800">
            Masuk
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  lucide.createIcons();
}

async function submitRoleSwitch(targetRole) {
  const store = window.store;
  const input = document.getElementById('role-auth-input');
  if (!input) return;
  const val = input.value.trim();

  const nameInput = document.getElementById('role-cashier-name-input');
  const cashierName = (nameInput && nameInput.value.trim()) ? nameInput.value.trim() : store.cashierName;

  const res = await store.login(targetRole, val, cashierName);
  if (res.success) {
    closeModal();
    if (targetRole === 'admin' && window._pendingRegisterBarcode) {
      const pendingCode = window._pendingRegisterBarcode;
      window._pendingRegisterBarcode = null;
      window.navigate('admin-products');
      openProductModal(null, pendingCode);
    } else {
      window.navigate(targetRole === 'admin' ? 'admin-dashboard' : 'pos');
    }
  } else {
    if (store.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playError();
    alert(res.message || (targetRole === 'admin' ? 'Password admin salah!' : 'PIN kasir salah!'));
  }
}

// ==================== HELD CARTS MODAL ====================
function openHeldCartsModal() {
  const store = window.store;
  const list = store.heldCarts;
  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-6 space-y-4">
        
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="clock" class="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500"></i>
            <h3 class="font-bold text-base text-slate-800 dark:text-white">Pesanan Tertunda (Hold)</h3>
          </div>
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600">
            <i data-lucide="x" class="w-5 h-5"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto space-y-2 divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800/60">
          ${list.length > 0 ? list.map(h => {
            const total = h.cart.reduce((a, b) => a + b.subtotal, 0) - (h.discount || 0);
            return `
              <div class="pt-2 flex items-center justify-between">

                  <h4 class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">${escapeHtml(h.note)}</h4>

                </div>
                <div class="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                  <button onclick="restoreHeld('${h.id}')" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow-xs">
                    Lanjutkan
                  </button>
                  <button onclick="deleteHeld('${h.id}')" class="p-1.5 text-rose-500 hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40 rounded-lg">
                    <i data-lucide="trash" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('') : `
            <div class="py-8 text-center text-slate-400">
              <p class="text-xs">Tidak ada pesanan tertunda.</p>
            </div>
          `}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  lucide.createIcons();
}

function restoreHeld(id) {
  window.store.restoreHeldCart(id);
  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
}

function deleteHeld(id) {
  window.store.deleteHeldCart(id);
  openHeldCartsModal();
}

// ==================== CAMERA SCANNER MODAL ====================
function stopActiveCameraStream() {
  if (window._activeCameraTimer) {
    clearInterval(window._activeCameraTimer);
    window._activeCameraTimer = null;
  }
  if (window._activeCameraStream) {
    try {
      window._activeCameraStream.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
    } catch (e) {}
    window._activeCameraStream = null;
  }
}

async function openCameraScannerModal() {
  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  stopActiveCameraStream();

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 p-6 space-y-4 text-center">
        
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <h3 class="font-bold text-sm text-slate-800 dark:text-white flex items-center space-x-2">
            <i data-lucide="camera" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600"></i>
            <span>Scan Barcode Kamera</span>
          </h3>
          <button onclick="closeModal()" class="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200">
            <i data-lucide="x" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="relative bg-slate-950 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden aspect-square flex items-center justify-center border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-500/50">
          <video id="camera-scanner-video" autoplay playsinline muted class="w-full h-full object-cover hidden"></video>
          <div id="camera-scanner-view" class="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-400 p-4">
            <i data-lucide="scan" class="w-16 h-16 text-emerald-400 animate-pulse mb-3"></i>
            <p id="camera-status-text" class="text-xs text-slate-300">Menghubungkan ke kamera perangkat...</p>
          </div>
          <div class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-6 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-0.5 bg-emerald-400/80 shadow-[0_0_8px_#10b981]"></div>
        </div>

        <!-- Manual Barcode Input inside Modal -->
        <div class="flex gap-2">
          <input
            type="text"
            id="modal-manual-barcode"
            placeholder="Ketik / tempel kode barcode..."
            onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){ event.preventDefault(); simulateScanBarcode(this.value.trim()); }"
            class="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono text-slate-800 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onclick="simulateScanBarcode(document.getElementById('modal-manual-barcode').value.trim())"
            class="px-3.5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold"
          >
            Cari
          </button>
        </div>

        ${window.store.products.length > 0 ? `
          <div class="space-y-2">
            <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Atau klik cepat barcode produk tersedia:</p>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 justify-center max-h-28 overflow-y-auto">
              ${window.store.products.slice(0, 8).map(p => `
                <button onclick="simulateScanBarcode('${escapeHtml(p.barcode)}')" class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono hover:bg-emerald-100 hover:text-emerald-700 dark:hover:bg-emerald-950 dark:hover:text-emerald-300 transition">
                  ${escapeHtml(p.barcode)} (${escapeHtml(p.name.slice(0, 12))})
                </button>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        ` : ''}

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  lucide.createIcons();

  // Attempt to activate device camera
  if (navigator.mediaDevices && navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: { facingMode: 'environment' }
      });
      window._activeCameraStream = stream;
      const videoEl = document.getElementById('camera-scanner-video');
      const placeholderEl = document.getElementById('camera-scanner-view');
      if (videoEl) {
        videoEl.srcObject = stream;
        videoEl.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (placeholderEl) placeholderEl.classList.add('hidden');
      }

      // Use BarcodeDetector API if supported by browser (Chrome/Edge/Android)
      if ('BarcodeDetector' in window) {
        const detector = new BarcodeDetector({ formats: ['ean_13', 'ean_8', 'code_128', 'code_39', 'qr_code', 'upc_a', 'upc_e'] });
        window._activeCameraTimer = setInterval(async () => {
          if (!videoEl || videoEl.readyState < 2) return;
          try {
            const barcodes = await detector.detect(videoEl);
            if (barcodes && barcodes.length > 0) {
              const code = barcodes[0].rawValue;
              if (code) {
                simulateScanBarcode(code);
              }
            }
          } catch (err) {}
        }, 350);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const statusText = document.getElementById('camera-status-text');
      if (statusText) {
        statusText.innerText = 'Kamera tidak diizinkan atau membutuhkan koneksi HTTPS. Gunakan input barcode di bawah.';
      }
    }
  } else {
    const statusText = document.getElementById('camera-status-text');
    if (statusText) {
      statusText.innerText = 'Kamera langsung membutuhkan localhost / HTTPS. Gunakan input atau tombol barcode di bawah.';
    }
  }
}

function simulateScanBarcode(barcode) {
  if (!barcode) return;
  const match = window.store.products.find(p => p.barcode === barcode || p.id.toLowerCase() === barcode.toLowerCase());
  if (match) {
    const res = window.store.addToCart(match.id, 1);
    if (!res.success) {
      alert(res.message);
      return;
    }
    closeModal();
    window.renderApp();
    window.store.showRealtimeToast(`âœ… ${match.name} ditambahkan ke keranjang!`);
  } else {
    if (window.store.settings.soundEnabled && typeof soundFx !== 'undefined') soundFx.playError();
    alert(`Produk dengan barcode "${barcode}" tidak ditemukan!`);
  }
}

function copyToClipboard(text, alertMsg) {
  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
      window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ“‹ ${alertMsg || "Berhasil disalin!"}`);
    }).catch(() => fallbackCopyText(text, alertMsg));
  } else {
    fallbackCopyText(text, alertMsg);
  }
}

function fallbackCopyText(text, alertMsg) {
  const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
  ta.value = text;
  ta.style.position = 'fixed';
  ta.style.opacity = '0';
  document.body.appendChild(ta);
  ta.focus();
  ta.select();
  try {
    document.execCommand('copy');
    window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸ“‹ ${alertMsg || "Berhasil disalin!"}`);
  } catch (e) {
    alert(alertMsg || `Salin manual: ${text}`);
  }
  document.body.removeChild(ta);
}

function closeModal() {
  stopActiveCameraStream();
  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (container) container.innerHTML = '';
}

// ==================== POS DISCOUNT & PROMO SELECTOR MODAL ====================
function openDiscountModalPos() {
  if (typeof isMobileCartOpen !== 'undefined' && isMobileCartOpen) {
    isMobileCartOpen = false;
    window.renderApp();
  }
  const store = window.store;
  const calc = store.getCartCalculations();
  const subtotal = calc.subtotal;
  const activeDiscounts = (store.discounts || []).filter(d => Boolean(d.isActive));
  const currentDiscount = store.activeDiscount;
  const currentInfo = store.activeDiscountInfo;

  const container = document.getElementById('modal-container');
  if (!container) return;

  container.innerHTML = `
    <div class="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 overflow-y-auto">
      <div class="bg-white dark:bg-slate-900 rounded-3xl w-full max-w-lg shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800 overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
        
        <!-- Header -->
        <div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div class="flex items-center space-x-2.5">

                              âš ï¸ Belanja kurang ${formatRupiah(deficit)} lagi untuk promo ini
                            </p>

                            <p class="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-semibold mt-0.5">

            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">Atau Masukkan Diskon Manual (Rp)</label>
              <span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-indigo-50 dark:bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400">

            Selesai
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  lucide.createIcons();
}

function handleApplyVoucherCode() {
  const input = document.getElementById('voucher-code-input');
  if (!input) return;
  const code = input.value.trim();
  if (!code) {
    alert("Silakan ketik kode voucher promo!");
    return;
  }

  const res = window.store.applyDiscountPromo(code);
  if (!res.success) {
    alert(res.message);
    return;
  }

  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸŽ‰ Promo "${res.name}" berhasil digunakan! Diskon: ${formatRupiah(res.discountAmount)}`);
}

function handleApplyDiscountPreset(discountId) {
  const res = window.store.applyDiscountPromo(discountId);
  if (!res.success) {
    alert(res.message);
    return;
  }

  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast(`ðŸŽ‰ Promo "${res.name}" aktif! Diskon: ${formatRupiah(res.discountAmount)}`);
}

async function handleApplyManualDiscount() {
  const input = document.getElementById('manual-discount-input');
  if (!input) return;
  const val = Math.max(0, Number(input.value) || 0);
  const store = window.store;

  if (val <= 0) {
    store.setDiscount(0, null);
    closeModal();
    window.renderApp();
    return;
  }

  let adminPass = '';
  if (store.currentRole !== 'admin') {
    const passInput = document.getElementById('manual-discount-admin-pass');
    adminPass = passInput ? passInput.value.trim() : '';
    if (!adminPass) {
      adminPass = (prompt('Diskon manual memerlukan otorisasi Admin.\nMasukkan Password Admin:') || '').trim();
    }
    if (!adminPass) {
      alert('Diskon manual dibatalkan karena Password Admin tidak diisi.');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await store.apiFetch('/api/auth/verify-admin', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ password: adminPass })
      });
      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok || !data.success) {
        alert(data.error || 'Password Admin salah! Diskon manual ditolak.');
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Gagal memverifikasi otorisasi Admin ke server.');
      return;
    }
  }

  store.setDiscount(val, {
    name: 'Diskon Manual',
    type: 'fixed',
    value: val,
    adminAuthPassword: adminPass || undefined
  });
  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
  store.showRealtimeToast(`âœ‚ï¸ Diskon manual ${formatRupiah(val)} berhasil diotorisasi & diterapkan.`);
}

function handleRemovePosDiscount() {
  window.store.removeDiscount();
  closeModal();
  window.renderApp();
  window.store.showRealtimeToast('Diskon telah dihapus dari keranjang.');
}
